🔬 NEXORA LAB — مختبر الفحص

بيفحص الاستراتيجيات على أسعار بايننس فيوتشرز الحقيقية جوّا المتصفح — بدون كمبيوتر ولا cmd. نفس محرّك المنصة بالضبط. ← رجوع للمنصة
جاهز. أول فحص على 30 عملة بياخد كم دقيقة (تحميل)، وبعدها البيانات بتنحفظ بالمتصفح وبيصير سريع.

النتيجة كنص

// ===================== NEXORA ENGINE (نفس منطق الفحص بالضبط) ===================== const M15 = 900000, H4 = 16 * M15; function aggregate(D, m) { const len = D.o.length, n = D.prov ? Math.ceil(len / m) : Math.floor(len / m), O = [], H = [], L = [], C = []; // D.prov = آخر شمعة 15m هي الشمعة الحية (لسا ما سكّرت) → آخر شمعة كبيرة ناقصة for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { O.push(D.o[i*m]); let hi = -Infinity, lo = Infinity; const end = Math.min((i+1)*m, len); for (let k = i*m; k < end; k++) { if (D.h[k] > hi) hi = D.h[k]; if (D.l[k] < lo) lo = D.l[k]; } H.push(hi); L.push(lo); C.push(D.c[end-1]); } return { O, H, L, C }; } function ema(C, p) { const k = 2/(p+1), out = []; for (let i = 0; i < C.length; i++) out.push(i === 0 ? C[0] : C[i]*k + out[i-1]*(1-k)); return out; } function makeInd(D, m) { const { O, H, L, C } = aggregate(D, m), n = C.length; const BLO = O.map((o, i) => Math.min(o, C[i])), BHI = O.map((o, i) => Math.max(o, C[i])); const tr = [H[0]-L[0]]; for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) tr.push(Math.max(H[i]-L[i], Math.abs(H[i]-C[i-1]), Math.abs(L[i]-C[i-1]))); const atr = []; let a = null; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { if (i === 13) { a = 0; for (let k = 0; k < 14; k++) a += tr[k]; a /= 14; } else if (i > 13) a = (a*13 + tr[i]) / 14; atr.push(a); } const pdm = [0], mdm = [0]; for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) { const up = H[i]-H[i-1], dn = L[i-1]-L[i]; pdm.push(up > dn ? Math.max(up, 0) : 0); mdm.push(dn > up ? Math.max(dn, 0) : 0); } const adx = new Array(n).fill(null); let smtr = 0, smp = 0, smm = 0; for (let k = 1; k < 15 && k < n; k++) { smtr += tr[k]; smp += pdm[k]; smm += mdm[k]; } let cnt = 0, first = 0; for (let i = 14; i < n; i++) { if (i > 14) { smtr = smtr - smtr/14 + tr[i]; smp = smp - smp/14 + pdm[i]; smm = smm - smm/14 + mdm[i]; } const pdi = smtr > 0 ? 100*smp/smtr : 0, mdi = smtr > 0 ? 100*smm/smtr : 0; const dx = (pdi + mdi) > 0 ? 100*Math.abs(pdi-mdi)/(pdi+mdi) : 0; cnt++; if (cnt <= 14) first += dx; if (cnt === 14) adx[i] = first/14; else if (cnt > 14) adx[i] = (adx[i-1]*13 + dx)/14; } return { m, n, O, H, L, C, BLO, BHI, atr, ema200: ema(C, 200), ema50: ema(C, 50), adx, partial: !!D.prov }; // partial = آخر شمعة لسا ما سكّرت (للدخول والخروج بس، مش للإشارات) } const minR = (a, s, e) => { let v = Infinity; for (let k = Math.max(0, s); k < e; k++) if (a[k] < v) v = a[k]; return v; }; const maxR = (a, s, e) => { let v = -Infinity; for (let k = Math.max(0, s); k < e; k++) if (a[k] > v) v = a[k]; return v; }; const sigV33 = k => (I, i) => I.C[i] > maxR(I.BHI, i-k, i) ? 1 : (I.C[i] < minR(I.BLO, i-k, i) ? -1 : 0); const sigShort = body => (I, i) => { if (I.adx[i] === null || I.atr[i] === null || I.atr[i] <= 0 || I.adx[i] <= 25) return 0; if (Math.abs(I.C[i]-I.O[i]) < body*I.atr[i]) return 0; return (I.C[i] < minR(I.BLO, i-4, i) && I.C[i] < I.ema200[i]) ? -1 : 0; }; const sigDip = () => (I, i) => { if (I.atr[i] === null || I.atr[i] <= 0) return 0; if (I.C[i] <= I.ema200[i] || I.C[i] <= I.ema50[i]) return 0; let dipped = false; for (let j = Math.max(0, i-3); j < i; j++) if (I.L[j] <= I.ema50[j]*1.005) dipped = true; return (dipped && I.C[i] > I.O[i] && (I.C[i]-I.O[i]) >= 0.3*I.atr[i]) ? 1 : 0; }; const sigGann43 = (lb, bm) => (I, i) => { if (I.atr[i] === null || I.atr[i] <= 0 || I.C[i] <= I.ema200[i]) return 0; return (I.BLO[i] === minR(I.BLO, i-lb, i+1) && (I.C[i]-I.O[i]) >= bm*I.atr[i]) ? 1 : 0; }; const sigGann44 = mn => (I, i) => { if (I.adx[i] === null || I.atr[i] === null || I.atr[i] <= 0) return 0; if (I.C[i] <= I.ema200[i] || I.adx[i] < mn) return 0; return (I.BLO[i] === minR(I.BLO, i-3, i+1) && (I.C[i]-I.O[i]) >= 0.4*I.atr[i]) ? 1 : 0; }; const sigDual = bm => (I, i) => { if (I.atr[i] === null || I.atr[i] <= 0 || Math.abs(I.C[i]-I.O[i]) < bm*I.atr[i]) return 0; if (I.BLO[i] === minR(I.BLO, i-2, i+1) && I.C[i] > I.O[i] && I.C[i] > I.ema50[i]) return 1; if (I.BHI[i] === maxR(I.BHI, i-2, i+1) && I.C[i] < I.O[i] && I.C[i] < I.ema50[i]) return -1; return 0; }; // دمج: SHORT من V42 (تحت EMA200) و LONG من Gann 3/.4 (فوق EMA200) — ما بيتعارضوا لأن شرط EMA200 عكس بعض const sigCombo = () => { const sh = sigShort(0), lg = sigGann43(3, 0.4); return (I, i) => sh(I, i) || lg(I, i); }; // ===================== Strategy 17: SSL Hybrid + QQE MOD + WAE (منقولة حرفياً من strategy17_math.py) ===================== const S17 = (() => { const fin = v => v !== null && v !== undefined && Number.isFinite(v); const CFG = { baselineLength: 60, continuationLength: 5, jurikPhase: 3, jurikPower: 1, exitLength: 15, atrPeriod: 14, qqe1RsiLength: 6, qqe1Smoothing: 6, qqe1Factor: 3, qqe1Threshold: 3, qqeBollingerLength: 50, qqeBollingerMultiplier: 0.35, qqe2RsiLength: 6, qqe2Smoothing: 5, qqe2Factor: 1.61, qqe2Threshold: 3, waeSensitivity: 180, waeFastLength: 20, waeSlowLength: 40, waeChannelLength: 20, waeBollingerMultiplier: 2, stopReferenceOffset: 2, stopBufferBps: 5 }; function rolling(v, n, fn) { const out = new Array(v.length).fill(null); for (let i = n - 1; i < v.length; i++) { let ok = true; for (let k = i - n + 1; k <= i; k++) if (!fin(v[k])) { ok = false; break; } if (ok) out[i] = fn(v, i - n + 1, i); } return out; } const sma = (v, n) => rolling(v, n, (a, s, e) => { let t = 0; for (let k = s; k <= e; k++) t += a[k]; return t / n; }); const stdev = (v, n) => rolling(v, n, (a, s, e) => { let t = 0; for (let k = s; k <= e; k++) t += a[k]; const m = t / n; let q = 0; for (let k = s; k <= e; k++) q += (a[k] - m) ** 2; return Math.sqrt(q / n); }); const wma = (v, n) => { const den = n * (n + 1) / 2; return rolling(v, n, (a, s, e) => { let t = 0; for (let k = s; k <= e; k++) t += a[k] * (k - s + 1); return t / den; }); }; function ema(v, n) { const out = new Array(v.length).fill(null), al = 2 / (n + 1); let p = null; for (let i = 0; i < v.length; i++) { if (!fin(v[i])) { p = null; continue; } p = p === null ? v[i] : al * v[i] + (1 - al) * p; out[i] = p; } return out; } function rma(v, n) { const out = new Array(v.length).fill(null); let seed = [], p = null; for (let i = 0; i < v.length; i++) { const x = v[i]; if (!fin(x)) { seed = []; p = null; continue; } if (p === null) { seed.push(x); if (seed.length === n) { p = seed.reduce((s, y) => s + y, 0) / n; out[i] = p; } } else { p = (p * (n - 1) + x) / n; out[i] = p; } } return out; } function hma(v, n) { const half = Math.max(1, Math.floor(n / 2)), root = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Math.sqrt(n) + 0.5)); const a = wma(v, half), b = wma(v, n); return wma(a.map((x, i) => fin(x) && fin(b[i]) ? 2 * x - b[i] : null), root); } function jma(v, n, phase, power) { const pr = phase < -100 ? 0.5 : phase > 100 ? 2.5 : phase / 100 + 1.5; const beta = n > 1 ? 0.45 * (n - 1) / (0.45 * (n - 1) + 2) : 0, alpha = beta ** power; const out = new Array(v.length).fill(null); let e0 = 0, e1 = 0, e2 = 0, prev = 0; for (let i = 0; i < v.length; i++) { if (!fin(v[i])) continue; const x = v[i]; e0 = (1 - alpha) * x + alpha * e0; e1 = (x - e0) * (1 - beta) + beta * e1; e2 = (e0 + pr * e1 - prev) * (1 - alpha) ** 2 + alpha ** 2 * e2; prev = e2 + prev; out[i] = prev; } return out; } function rsi(c, n) { const g = new Array(c.length).fill(null), l = new Array(c.length).fill(null); for (let i = 1; i < c.length; i++) { const d = c[i] - c[i - 1]; g[i] = Math.max(0, d); l[i] = Math.max(0, -d); } const ag = rma(g, n), al = rma(l, n); return ag.map((x, i) => fin(x) && fin(al[i]) ? (al[i] === 0 ? 100 : x === 0 ? 0 : 100 - 100 / (1 + x / al[i])) : null); } const crossUp = (a, b, i) => i > 0 && fin(a[i-1]) && fin(b[i-1]) && fin(a[i]) && fin(b[i]) && a[i-1] <= b[i-1] && a[i] > b[i]; const crossDn = (a, b, i) => i > 0 && fin(a[i-1]) && fin(b[i-1]) && fin(a[i]) && fin(b[i]) && a[i-1] >= b[i-1] && a[i] < b[i]; function qqe(c, rl, sm, fac) { const raw = rsi(c, rl), s = ema(raw, sm), delta = new Array(c.length).fill(null); for (let i = 1; i < c.length; i++) if (fin(s[i]) && fin(s[i-1])) delta[i] = Math.abs(s[i] - s[i-1]); const w = rl * 2 - 1, dar = ema(ema(delta, w), w).map(x => fin(x) ? x * fac : null); const L = new Array(c.length).fill(null), Sh = new Array(c.length).fill(null), trail = new Array(c.length).fill(null); let ts = 1; for (let i = 0; i < c.length; i++) { if (!fin(s[i]) || !fin(dar[i])) continue; const cur = s[i], nl = cur - dar[i], ns = cur + dar[i]; const pl = i ? L[i-1] : null, ps = i ? Sh[i-1] : null, prs = i ? s[i-1] : null; L[i] = fin(pl) && fin(prs) && prs > pl && cur > pl ? Math.max(pl, nl) : nl; Sh[i] = fin(ps) && fin(prs) && prs < ps && cur < ps ? Math.min(ps, ns) : ns; if (i >= 2 && [s[i-1], s[i], Sh[i-2], Sh[i-1], L[i-2], L[i-1]].every(fin)) { const cs = s[i-1] <= Sh[i-2] && s[i] > Sh[i-1]; const cl = (L[i-2] <= s[i-1] && L[i-1] > s[i]) || (L[i-2] >= s[i-1] && L[i-1] < s[i]); if (cs) ts = 1; else if (cl) ts = -1; } trail[i] = ts === 1 ? L[i] : Sh[i]; } return { smoothed: s, trail }; } // المؤشرات على شموع الفريم (O,H,L,C) function calc(O, H, Lo, C) { const n = C.length, J = [CFG.jurikPhase, CFG.jurikPower]; const base = hma(C, CFG.baselineLength), bH = hma(H, CFG.baselineLength), bL = hma(Lo, CFG.baselineLength); const cH = jma(H, CFG.continuationLength, ...J), cL = jma(Lo, CFG.continuationLength, ...J); const xH = hma(H, CFG.exitLength), xL = hma(Lo, CFG.exitLength); const tr = C.map((_, i) => i ? Math.max(H[i] - Lo[i], Math.abs(H[i] - C[i-1]), Math.abs(Lo[i] - C[i-1])) : H[i] - Lo[i]); const atr = wma(tr, CFG.atrPeriod); const bs = [], exitLine = new Array(n).fill(null); let bd = 0, ed = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { if (fin(bH[i]) && C[i] > bH[i]) bd = 1; else if (fin(bL[i]) && C[i] < bL[i]) bd = -1; if (fin(xH[i]) && C[i] > xH[i]) ed = 1; else if (fin(xL[i]) && C[i] < xL[i]) ed = -1; bs.push(bd); exitLine[i] = ed < 0 ? xH[i] : ed > 0 ? xL[i] : null; } const arrowUp = new Array(n).fill(false), arrowDn = new Array(n).fill(false); for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) { if (fin(exitLine[i-1]) && fin(exitLine[i]) && crossUp(C, exitLine, i)) arrowUp[i] = true; if (fin(exitLine[i-1]) && fin(exitLine[i]) && crossDn(C, exitLine, i)) arrowDn[i] = true; } const q1 = qqe(C, CFG.qqe1RsiLength, CFG.qqe1Smoothing, CFG.qqe1Factor), q2 = qqe(C, CFG.qqe2RsiLength, CFG.qqe2Smoothing, CFG.qqe2Factor); const fc = q1.trail.map(x => fin(x) ? x - 50 : null), bb = sma(fc, CFG.qqeBollingerLength), bd2 = stdev(fc, CFG.qqeBollingerLength); const up = bb.map((b, i) => fin(b) && fin(bd2[i]) ? b + bd2[i] * CFG.qqeBollingerMultiplier : null); const lw = bb.map((b, i) => fin(b) && fin(bd2[i]) ? b - bd2[i] * CFG.qqeBollingerMultiplier : null); const qh = q2.smoothed.map(x => fin(x) ? x - 50 : null), q1h = q1.smoothed.map(x => fin(x) ? x - 50 : null); const qL = qh.map((x, i) => fin(x) && x > CFG.qqe2Threshold && fin(q1h[i]) && q1h[i] > CFG.qqe1Threshold && fin(up[i]) && q1h[i] > up[i]); const qS = qh.map((x, i) => fin(x) && x < -CFG.qqe2Threshold && fin(q1h[i]) && q1h[i] < -CFG.qqe1Threshold && fin(lw[i]) && q1h[i] < lw[i]); const mf = ema(C, CFG.waeFastLength), ms = ema(C, CFG.waeSlowLength), macd = mf.map((a, i) => fin(a) && fin(ms[i]) ? a - ms[i] : null); const wu = new Array(n).fill(null), wd = new Array(n).fill(null); for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) if (fin(macd[i]) && fin(macd[i-1])) { const d = (macd[i] - macd[i-1]) * CFG.waeSensitivity; wu[i] = Math.max(d, 0); wd[i] = Math.max(-d, 0); } const bw = stdev(C, CFG.waeChannelLength), expl = bw.map(d => fin(d) ? 2 * CFG.waeBollingerMultiplier * d : null); const long = new Array(n).fill(false), short = new Array(n).fill(false), ready = new Array(n).fill(false); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { ready[i] = [base[i], exitLine[i], atr[i], fc[i], qh[i], wu[i], wd[i], expl[i]].every(fin); long[i] = ready[i] && C[i] > O[i] && C[i] > base[i] && bs[i] === 1 && qL[i] && wu[i] > expl[i]; short[i] = ready[i] && C[i] < O[i] && C[i] < base[i] && bs[i] === -1 && qS[i] && wd[i] > expl[i]; } return { ready, long, short, arrowUp, arrowDn, base, exitLine }; } const memo = new WeakMap(); const get = I => { if (!memo.has(I)) memo.set(I, calc(I.O, I.H, I.L, I.C)); return memo.get(I); }; // إشارة (للرادار) const sig = (I, i) => { const X = get(I); return X.long[i] ? 1 : X.short[i] ? -1 : 0; }; // تشغيل الصفقات بنفس منطق replay_strategy17 (دخول فتح الشمعة الجاية، وقف على شمعة i-2، خروج بالوقف أو سهم خروج SSL) function run(D, inds, sp, startTs) { const m = sp.mult, I = inds[m], X = get(I), n = I.n, out = [], buf = CFG.stopBufferBps / 10000; let pos = null, pend = null; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { let closedNow = false; if (pend) { const p = pend; pend = null; const entry = I.O[i], sign = p.d; if (sign * (entry - p.stop) > 0) pos = { d: p.d, e: i, entry, stop: p.stop, stop0: p.stop, best: entry, target: sp.tgtR ? entry + sign*sp.tgtR*Math.abs(entry - p.stop) : NaN, ...(sp.riskPct ? { w: Math.min(sp.wMax || 3, sp.riskPct/(100*Math.abs(entry - p.stop)/entry)) } : {}) }; } if (pos) { const sign = pos.d, gap = sign === 1 ? I.O[i] <= pos.stop : I.O[i] >= pos.stop, rng = sign === 1 ? I.L[i] <= pos.stop : I.H[i] >= pos.stop; let raw = null, why = null; if (gap || rng) { raw = gap ? I.O[i] : pos.stop; why = "INIT_STOP"; } else if (sp.tgtR && (sign === 1 ? I.H[i] >= pos.target : I.L[i] <= pos.target)) { raw = sign*(I.O[i] - pos.target) >= 0 ? I.O[i] : pos.target; why = "TARGET"; } else if ((sign === 1 ? X.arrowDn[i] : X.arrowUp[i]) && i >= pos.e) { raw = I.C[i]; why = "SSL_EXIT"; } if (raw !== null) { if (why === "INIT_STOP" && pos.stop !== pos.stop0) why = "TRAIL"; out.push({ name: sp.name, d: pos.d, e: pos.e*m, x: i*m + m - 1, rawIn: pos.entry, rawOut: raw, why, open: false, stop: pos.stop, target: pos.target, ...(pos.w ? { w: pos.w } : {}) }); pos = null; closedNow = true; } else if (sp.trailAtr && I.atr[i]) { // وقف متحرك بعد ما تربح trailFrom×R (بيطبق من الشمعة الجاية) pos.best = sign === 1 ? Math.max(pos.best, I.H[i]) : Math.min(pos.best, I.L[i]); if (sign*(pos.best - pos.entry) >= (sp.trailFrom || 1)*Math.abs(pos.entry - pos.stop0)) { const t = pos.best - sign*sp.trailAtr*I.atr[i]; if (sign*(t - pos.stop) > 0) pos.stop = t; } } } if (!closedNow && !pos && i + 1 <= n && D.t0 + (i+1)*m*M15 > startTs && X.ready[i] && i >= 2) { const d = X.long[i] ? 1 : X.short[i] ? -1 : 0; if (d) { const ref = i - CFG.stopReferenceOffset; let stop = d === 1 ? I.L[ref] * (1 - buf) : I.H[ref] * (1 + buf); if (sp.atrStop && I.atr[i]) stop = I.C[i] - d*sp.atrStop*I.atr[i]; // نسخ محسّنة: وقف ATR const trendOk = !sp.trend || (I.ema200[i] !== null && I.ema200[i] !== undefined && (d === 1 ? I.C[i] > I.ema200[i] : I.C[i] < I.ema200[i])); if (trendOk && (i + 1) * m < D.o.length) pend = { d, stop }; } } } if (pos) out.push({ name: sp.name, d: pos.d, e: pos.e*m, x: D.o.length - 1, rawIn: pos.entry, rawOut: D.c[D.c.length-1], why: "TIME", open: true, stop: pos.stop, target: pos.target, ...(pos.w ? { w: pos.w } : {}) }); return out; } return { calc, sig, run, CFG }; })(); // ===================== Strategy 16: SMC Supply & Demand + Liquidity Sweep (منقولة من smc/engine.py + paper.py) ===================== const S16 = (() => { const TFM = { "15m": 900000, "1h": 3600000, "4h": 14400000 }; const CFG = { atrPeriod: 14, displacementAtr: 0.8, maxZoneWidthAtr: 3, baseMaxWidthAtr: 1, maxZoneAgeBars: 120, interactionBars: 8, reversalBars: 3, allowTestedZones: false, maxTouches: 3 }; const PORTS = [["16A-1h", "A", "1h"], ["16A-4h", "A", "4h"], ["16B-1h", "B", "1h"], ["16B-4h", "B", "4h"]]; const id = (...v) => v.map(x => Array.isArray(x) ? "[" + x.join(",") + "]" : String(x)).join("|"); const TERM = new Set(["PAPER_POSITION_OPEN", "POSITION_CLOSED", "SETUP_EXPIRED", "ZONE_INVALIDATED", "SIGNAL_REJECTED", "DATA_UNAVAILABLE"]); const terminal = x => TERM.has(x.state); const transition = (setup, status, at, reason) => { setup.state = status; setup.updatedAt = at; if (reason) setup.reason = reason; }; const blank = (sym) => ({ symbol: sym, frames: {}, zones: new Map(), setups: new Map(), setupEpoch: 0 }); function frameStep(s, tf, b) { let f = s.frames[tf]; if (!f) f = s.frames[tf] = { bars: [], count: 0, atr: null, trSum: 0, highs: [], lows: [], broken: [] }; const prior = f.bars.length ? f.bars[f.bars.length-1].close : b.open; const tr = Math.max(b.high - b.low, Math.abs(b.high - prior), Math.abs(b.low - prior)); f.count++; const n = CFG.atrPeriod; if (f.count <= n) { f.trSum += tr; if (f.count === n) f.atr = f.trSum / n; } else f.atr = (f.atr * (n - 1) + tr) / n; return f; } function confirmSwings(s, f, b) { const N = f.bars.length + 1; if (N < 7) return; // 18/11: بدون نسخ المصفوفة كل شمعة (نفس النتيجة) const g = i => i === N - 1 ? b : f.bars[i]; const pivot = g(N-4), nb = [g(N-7), g(N-6), g(N-5), g(N-3), g(N-2), g(N-1)]; for (const [side, field, cmp] of [["high", "high", (a, c) => a > c], ["low", "low", (a, c) => a < c]]) { if (nb.every(x => cmp(pivot[field], x[field]))) { const point = { id: id(s.symbol, side, pivot.openTime), price: pivot[field], openTime: pivot.openTime, confirmedAt: b.closeTime, atrAtConfirmation: f.atr }; const key = side === "high" ? "highs" : "lows"; f[key].push(point); f[key] = f[key].slice(-24); } } } function baseFor(bars, side, atr) { if (!bars.length) return null; const opposite = b => side === "LONG" ? b.close < b.open : b.close > b.open; for (let size = Math.min(3, bars.length); size > 1; size--) { const part = bars.slice(-size); if (Math.max(...part.map(x => x.high)) - Math.min(...part.map(x => x.low)) <= CFG.baseMaxWidthAtr * atr && part.every(x => Math.abs(x.close - x.open) <= .5 * atr)) return part; } return opposite(bars[bars.length-1]) ? bars.slice(-1) : null; } function makeZone(s, tf, side, base, b, atr) { const lower = Math.min(...base.map(x => x.low)), upper = Math.max(...base.map(x => x.high)); if (!(0 < upper - lower && upper - lower <= CFG.maxZoneWidthAtr * atr)) return null; const key = id(s.symbol, tf, side, base[0].openTime, base[base.length-1].closeTime); if (!s.zones.has(key)) s.zones.set(key, { id: key, timeframe: tf, side, lower, upper, baseAt: base[0].openTime, createdAt: b.closeTime, confirmedAt: null, status: "pending", touchCount: 0, inside: false }); return s.zones.get(key); } function zoneStep(s, tf, f, b) { const atr = f.atr; if (!atr || atr <= 0) return; for (const z of s.zones.values()) { if (z.timeframe !== tf || z.status !== "pending") continue; if (b.closeTime - z.createdAt > 3 * TFM[tf]) { z.status = "expired"; z.endedAt = b.closeTime; } } for (const [side, levels] of [["LONG", f.highs], ["SHORT", f.lows]]) { const el = levels.filter(p => p.confirmedAt < b.openTime); if (!el.length) continue; const p = el[el.length-1]; const broken = side === "LONG" ? b.close > p.price : b.close < p.price; if (!broken || f.broken.includes(p.id)) continue; f.broken = f.broken.concat([p.id]).slice(-64); const directional = side === "LONG" ? b.close > b.open : b.close < b.open; if (!directional || Math.abs(b.close - b.open) < CFG.displacementAtr * atr) continue; const base = baseFor(f.bars, side, atr); if (!base) continue; const z = makeZone(s, tf, side, base, b, atr); if (z && z.status === "pending") { z.status = "fresh"; z.confirmedAt = b.closeTime; z.bosLevel = p.price; } } for (const side of ["LONG", "SHORT"]) { const base = baseFor(f.bars.concat([b]), side, atr); if (base) makeZone(s, tf, side, base, b, atr); } } function liquidity(f, side, b, atr) { const levels = f[side === "LONG" ? "lows" : "highs"].filter(p => p.confirmedAt < b.openTime); if (!levels.length) return null; const latest = levels[levels.length-1], defAtr = latest.atrAtConfirmation || atr; const equals = levels.slice(0, -1).filter(p => Math.abs(p.price - latest.price) <= .2 * defAtr); const prices = equals.map(p => p.price).concat([latest.price]); return { price: side === "LONG" ? Math.min(...prices) : Math.max(...prices), confirmedAt: Math.max(...equals.map(p => p.confirmedAt).concat([latest.confirmedAt])), referenceIds: equals.map(p => p.id).concat([latest.id]) }; } function waiting(s, z, port, v, at) { const number = z.touchCount + 1, key = id(z.id, port, number, s.setupEpoch); if (!s.setups.has(key)) { const x = { id: key, zoneId: z.id, portfolioId: port, version: v, side: z.side, touchNumber: number, touchedAt: null, expiresAt: z.confirmedAt + CFG.maxZoneAgeBars * TFM[z.timeframe], interactionExpiresAt: null, sweepId: null, state: "WAITING_FOR_ZONE_RETEST" }; s.setups.set(key, x); transition(x, "WAITING_FOR_ZONE_RETEST", at); } return s.setups.get(key); } function armWaiting(s, at) { for (const z of s.zones.values()) { if (!(z.status === "fresh" || z.status === "tested") || z.inside) continue; if (z.touchCount && (!CFG.allowTestedZones || z.touchCount >= CFG.maxTouches)) continue; for (const [port, v, tf] of PORTS) if (tf === z.timeframe) waiting(s, z, port, v, at); } } function interactionStep(s, f, b, live) { const atr = f.atr; for (const z of s.zones.values()) { if (!(z.status === "fresh" || z.status === "tested")) continue; const at = b.closeTime; const invalid = z.side === "LONG" ? b.close < z.lower : b.close > z.upper; const expired = at - z.confirmedAt >= CFG.maxZoneAgeBars * TFM[z.timeframe]; if (invalid || expired) { z.status = invalid ? "invalidated" : "expired"; z.endedAt = at; for (const x of s.setups.values()) if (x.zoneId === z.id && !terminal(x)) transition(x, invalid ? "ZONE_INVALIDATED" : "SETUP_EXPIRED", at, "zone"); continue; } if (b.openTime <= z.confirmedAt) continue; if (live && !z.inside) { if (!z.touchCount || (CFG.allowTestedZones && z.touchCount < CFG.maxTouches)) for (const [port, v, tf] of PORTS) if (tf === z.timeframe) waiting(s, z, port, v, at); } const inside = b.low <= z.upper && b.high >= z.lower, newTouch = inside && !z.inside; if (newTouch) { const previous = z.touchCount; z.touchCount++; z.status = "tested"; z.lastTouchAt = at; if (live) for (const [port, v, tf] of PORTS) { if (tf !== z.timeframe) continue; const key = id(z.id, port, z.touchCount, s.setupEpoch); const x = { id: key, zoneId: z.id, portfolioId: port, version: v, side: z.side, touchNumber: z.touchCount, touchedAt: at, expiresAt: at + (CFG.interactionBars - 1) * TFM["15m"], sweepId: null, state: "WAITING_FOR_ZONE_RETEST" }; x.interactionExpiresAt = x.expiresAt; s.setups.set(key, x); transition(x, "ZONE_TOUCHED", at); if (previous > 0 && (!CFG.allowTestedZones || z.touchCount > CFG.maxTouches)) transition(x, "SIGNAL_REJECTED", at, "tested"); else transition(x, "WAITING_FOR_LIQUIDITY_SWEEP", at); } } z.inside = inside; } if (!live || !atr || atr <= 0) return []; const entries = []; for (const x of s.setups.values()) { // 18/11: بدون نسخ (اللوب ما بيضيف ولا بيشيل) if (terminal(x)) continue; const z = s.zones.get(x.zoneId); if (!z || !(z.status === "fresh" || z.status === "tested")) continue; if (x.state === "WAITING_FOR_ZONE_RETEST") continue; if (b.closeTime > x.expiresAt) { transition(x, "SETUP_EXPIRED", b.closeTime, "window"); continue; } if (x.touchNumber !== z.touchCount) { transition(x, "SIGNAL_REJECTED", b.closeTime, "retest"); continue; } const long = x.side === "LONG"; if (x.sweepId && b.closeTime > x.sweepAt) { if (long ? b.low < x.sweepExtreme : b.high > x.sweepExtreme) { transition(x, "SIGNAL_REJECTED", b.closeTime, "breach"); continue; } } if (x.state === "WAITING_FOR_LIQUIDITY_SWEEP") { const level = liquidity(f, x.side, b, atr); const inside = b.low <= z.upper && b.high >= z.lower; const swept = level && (long ? b.low <= level.price - .1 * atr : b.high >= level.price + .1 * atr); if (!(inside && swept)) continue; Object.assign(x, { sweepId: id(s.symbol, x.side, b.openTime, level.referenceIds), sweepAt: b.closeTime, sweepExtreme: long ? b.low : b.high, liquidity: level, sweepAtr: atr }); if (x.version === "B") x.expiresAt = b.closeTime + 8 * TFM["15m"]; transition(x, "SWEEP_DETECTED", b.closeTime); } if (x.state === "SWEEP_DETECTED") { if (b.closeTime > x.interactionExpiresAt || b.closeTime - x.sweepAt > CFG.reversalBars * TFM["15m"]) { transition(x, "SETUP_EXPIRED", b.closeTime, "rev"); continue; } const reclaim = long ? b.close > x.liquidity.price : b.close < x.liquidity.price; const dirc = long ? b.close > b.open : b.close < b.open; const body = Math.abs(b.close - b.open), span = b.high - b.low; if (!(reclaim && dirc && span > 0 && body / span >= .5)) continue; x.reversalAt = b.closeTime; transition(x, "REVERSAL_CONFIRMED", b.closeTime); if (x.version === "B") transition(x, "WAITING_FOR_CHOCH", b.closeTime); else { x.confirmedAt = b.closeTime; x.entryAtr = atr; transition(x, "ENTRY_ELIGIBLE", b.closeTime); entries.push({ ...x }); continue; } } if (x.state === "WAITING_FOR_CHOCH") { if (b.closeTime - x.sweepAt > 8 * TFM["15m"]) { transition(x, "SETUP_EXPIRED", b.closeTime, "choch"); continue; } const opp = f[long ? "highs" : "lows"].filter(p => p.confirmedAt < b.openTime); if (!opp.length || b.closeTime <= x.sweepAt) continue; const ref = opp[opp.length-1], prev = f.bars[f.bars.length-1].close; const crossed = long ? (prev <= ref.price && ref.price < b.close) : (prev >= ref.price && ref.price > b.close); if (crossed) { Object.assign(x, { confirmedAt: b.closeTime, entryAtr: atr, chochLevel: ref.price, chochReferenceAt: ref.confirmedAt }); transition(x, "ENTRY_ELIGIBLE", b.closeTime); entries.push({ ...x }); } } } return entries; } function step(s, tf, b, live) { const f = frameStep(s, tf, b); let entries = []; if (tf === "1h" || tf === "4h") zoneStep(s, tf, f, b); else entries = interactionStep(s, f, b, live); if ((tf === "1h" || tf === "4h") && live) armWaiting(s, b.closeTime); confirmSwings(s, f, b); f.bars.push(b); const keep = Math.max(60, CFG.atrPeriod + 8); if (f.bars.length > keep) f.bars.splice(0, f.bars.length - keep); // 18/11: بدون نسخ (نفس النتيجة) for (const [k, z] of s.zones) if (!(z.status === "pending" || z.status === "fresh" || z.status === "tested" || b.closeTime - (z.endedAt ?? b.closeTime) < 4 * TFM["4h"])) s.zones.delete(k); for (const [k, x] of s.setups) if (!(!terminal(x) || b.closeTime - x.updatedAt < 4 * TFM["4h"])) s.setups.delete(k); return entries; } // تشغيل كامل على البيانات: 4 محافظ مستقلة، دخول على فتح الشمعة الجاية، وقف = طرف السويب ∓ 0.25 ATR، هدف 2R، أقصى 48 شمعة 15m const cache = new WeakMap(); function runAll(D, inds, startTs) { if (D.prov) { // S16 آلة حالات: بتشتغل بس على شموع مسكّرة if (!D._np) { const k = D.o.length - 1; D._np = { t0: D.t0, sym: D.sym, o: D.o.slice(0, k), h: D.h.slice(0, k), l: D.l.slice(0, k), c: D.c.slice(0, k), v: D.v && D.v.slice(0, k) }; D._npI = { 1: makeInd(D._np, 1), 4: makeInd(D._np, 4), 16: makeInd(D._np, 16) }; } const np = D._np, ni = D._npI; D = np; inds = ni; } const key = inds[1]; if (cache.has(key) && cache.get(key).startTs === startTs) return cache.get(key).out; const s = blank("BTCUSDT"), mk = (I, m, k) => ({ openTime: D.t0 + k*m*900000, closeTime: D.t0 + (k+1)*m*900000 - 1, open: I.O[k], high: I.H[k], low: I.L[k], close: I.C[k] }); const ev = []; for (const [m, tf, ord] of [[16, "4h", 0], [4, "1h", 1], [1, "15m", 2]]) { const I = inds[m]; for (let k = 0; k < I.n; k++) ev.push([D.t0 + (k+1)*m*900000 - 1, ord, tf, m, k]); } ev.sort((a, b) => (a[0] - b[0]) || (a[1] - b[1])); const out = {}, open = {}, used = {}; for (const [p] of PORTS) { out[p] = []; open[p] = null; used[p] = new Set(); } let armed = false; const I15 = inds[1]; for (const [ct, , tf, m, k] of ev) { const live = ct >= startTs; if (live && !armed) { armWaiting(s, ct); armed = true; } // إدارة الصفقات المفتوحة على شمعة 15m قبل ما الشمعة تدخل المحرّك if (tf === "15m") for (const [p] of PORTS) { const pos = open[p]; if (!pos || k < pos.e) continue; const d = pos.d, o = I15.O[k], h = I15.H[k], l = I15.L[k], c = I15.C[k]; let raw = null, why = null; if (d === 1 ? l <= pos.stop : h >= pos.stop) { raw = d === 1 ? Math.min(o, pos.stop) : Math.max(o, pos.stop); why = "INIT_STOP"; } else if (d === 1 ? h >= pos.target : l <= pos.target) { raw = d === 1 ? Math.max(o, pos.target) : Math.min(o, pos.target); why = "TARGET"; } else if (k >= pos.e + 47) { raw = c; why = "TIME"; } if (raw !== null) { out[p].push({ d, e: pos.e, x: k, rawIn: pos.entry, rawOut: raw, why, open: false, stop: pos.stop, target: pos.target }); open[p] = null; const st = s.setups.get(pos.setupId); if (st) transition(st, "POSITION_CLOSED", ct); } } const I = inds[m], bar = mk(I, m, k); const cands = step(s, tf, bar, live); for (const cnd of cands) { const p = cnd.portfolioId, setup = s.setups.get(cnd.id), e = k + 1; const z = s.zones.get(cnd.zoneId); const reject = () => { if (setup) transition(setup, "SIGNAL_REJECTED", ct, "rejected"); }; if (e >= I15.n || !z || !(z.status === "fresh" || z.status === "tested") || !setup || setup.state !== "ENTRY_ELIGIBLE" || open[p] || used[p].has(cnd.sweepId)) { reject(); continue; } const d = cnd.side === "LONG" ? 1 : -1, entry = I15.O[e]; if (d === 1 ? entry <= cnd.sweepExtreme : entry >= cnd.sweepExtreme) { reject(); continue; } const stop = cnd.sweepExtreme - d * .25 * cnd.entryAtr, dist = d * (entry - stop); if (!(stop > 0 && dist > 0)) { reject(); continue; } open[p] = { d, e, entry, stop, target: entry + d * 2 * dist, setupId: cnd.id }; used[p].add(cnd.sweepId); transition(setup, "PAPER_POSITION_OPEN", ct); } } for (const [p] of PORTS) if (open[p]) { const pos = open[p]; out[p].push({ d: pos.d, e: pos.e, x: I15.n - 1, rawIn: pos.entry, rawOut: I15.C[I15.n-1], why: "TIME", open: true, stop: pos.stop, target: pos.target }); } cache.set(key, { startTs, out }); return out; } const strat = (port, label) => ({ name: "S16 SMC " + label, mult: 1, sm: 0, tm: 0, hold: 48, trail: null, cd: null, entry: "imm", src: "Replit #16", sig: () => 0, run: (D, inds, sp, startTs) => runAll(D, inds, startTs)[port].filter(t => D.t0 + t.e*900000 >= startTs).map(t => ({ ...t, name: sp.name })), }); return { blank, step, armWaiting, runAll, CFG, PORTS, strats: [strat("16A-1h", "A 1h"), strat("16A-4h", "A 4h"), strat("16B-1h", "B 1h (CHoCH)"), strat("16B-4h", "B 4h (CHoCH)")] }; })(); // اتجاه البيتكوين (للفلتر): شموع 4 ساعات من بيانات BTC، EMA بفترة p على الـ4 ساعات. 1 فوق، -1 تحت، 0 مش معروف let BTC_REF = null; function setBtcRef(D) { if (!D) { BTC_REF = null; return; } const H4 = 14400000, C = [], T = []; let g0 = Math.ceil(D.t0/H4)*H4; for (let t = g0; t + H4 <= D.t0 + D.c.length*900000; t += H4) { const i = (t + H4 - D.t0)/900000 - 1; C.push(D.c[i]); T.push(t + H4); } BTC_REF = { C, T, ema: {} }; } function btcTrend(ts, p) { // ts = وقت إغلاق شمعة الإشارة const B = BTC_REF; if (!B) return 0; if (!B.ema[p]) { const out = new Array(B.C.length).fill(null); let e = null; for (let k = p - 1; k < B.C.length; k++) { if (e === null) { e = 0; for (let j = 0; j < p; j++) e += B.C[j]; e /= p; } else e = B.C[k]*(2/(p+1)) + e*((p-1)/(p+1)); out[k] = e; } B.ema[p] = out; } let lo = 0, hi = B.T.length - 1, j = -1; while (lo <= hi) { const md = (lo + hi) >> 1; if (B.T[md] <= ts) { j = md; lo = md + 1; } else hi = md - 1; } if (j < 0 || B.ema[p][j] === null) return 0; return B.C[j] > B.ema[p][j] ? 1 : -1; } // نوافذ فيبو الزمنية (4 ساعات): من آخر قمتين وآخر قاعين ورا بعض (Pivots 10/10 مؤكدة لحد k)، // النافذة = آخر نقطة + round(r × المسافة بينهم) ± 2 شمعة. set "A" = 0.618/1/1.618/2.618، "B" = 1/2/3/5/8 const FIBT_MEMO = new WeakMap(); function fibTimeWin(I, k, set) { if (!FIBT_MEMO.has(I)) FIBT_MEMO.set(I, {}); const M = FIBT_MEMO.get(I); if (!M[set]) { const n = I.n, H = I.H, L = I.L, LR = 10, out = new Uint8Array(n), ph = [], pl = [], rs = set === "B" ? [1, 2, 3, 5, 8] : [0.618, 1, 1.618, 2.618]; const isP = (p, hi) => { if (p - LR < 0 || p + LR >= n) return false; const v = hi ? H[p] : L[p]; for (let i = 1; i <= LR; i++) { const a = hi ? H[p-i] : L[p-i], b = hi ? H[p+i] : L[p+i]; if (hi ? !(v > a && v >= b) : !(v < a && v <= b)) return false; } return true; }; for (let t = 0; t < n; t++) { if (t - LR >= 0 && isP(t - LR, true)) ph.push(t - LR); if (t - LR >= 0 && isP(t - LR, false)) pl.push(t - LR); for (const P of [ph, pl]) { if (P.length < 2) continue; const p1 = P[P.length - 2], p2 = P[P.length - 1], N = p2 - p1; for (const r of rs) if (Math.abs(t - (p2 + Math.round(r*N))) <= 2) out[t] = 1; } } M[set] = out; } return M[set][k] === 1; } // ===================== Strategy 15: Liquidity Sweep + MSS (منقولة من sweep_rules.py + sweep_replay.py) ===================== const S15 = (() => { function prepare(I) { const n = I.n, atr = new Array(n).fill(null), ema = new Array(n).fill(null), events = new Map(); const tr = [0]; for (let k = 1; k < n; k++) tr.push(Math.max(I.H[k] - I.L[k], Math.abs(I.H[k] - I.C[k-1]), Math.abs(I.L[k] - I.C[k-1]))); let a = null, e = null; for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) { if (k >= 14) { if (a === null) { a = 0; for (let j = 1; j < 15; j++) a += tr[j]; a /= 14; } else a = (a*13 + tr[k]) / 14; atr[k] = a; } if (k >= 199) { if (e === null) { e = 0; for (let j = 0; j < 200; j++) e += I.C[j]; e /= 200; } else e = I.C[k]*(2/201) + e*(199/201); ema[k] = e; } } for (const side of [1, -1]) { let level = null, pending = null; for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) { if (level !== null && atr[k] !== null && k >= 3) { const sweep = side === 1 ? (level - I.L[k] >= .1*atr[k] && I.C[k] > level) : (I.H[k] - level >= .1*atr[k] && I.C[k] < level); if (sweep) { const th = side === 1 ? Math.max(I.H[k-3], I.H[k-2], I.H[k-1]) : Math.min(I.L[k-3], I.L[k-2], I.L[k-1]); pending = [k, th, level]; } else if (pending && (I.C[k] - pending[1])*side > 0 && k - pending[0] >= 1 && k - pending[0] <= 5) { const s = pending[0], stop = side === 1 ? I.L[s] - .25*atr[k] : I.H[s] + .25*atr[k]; if (!events.has(k)) events.set(k, []); events.get(k).push({ side, sweepIndex: s, stopLoss: stop, base: side === 1 ? I.L[s] : I.H[s], atrk: atr[k], depth: side === 1 ? (pending[2] - I.L[s])/atr[k] : (I.H[s] - pending[2])/atr[k] }); pending = null; } } if (pending && k - pending[0] > 5) pending = null; const i = k - 3; if (i >= 3) { const arr = side === 1 ? I.L : I.H; let ok = true; for (let j = i - 3; j <= k; j++) if (j !== i && !((arr[j] - arr[i])*side > 0)) { ok = false; break; } if (ok) level = arr[i]; } } } return { atr, ema, events }; } const memo = new WeakMap(); const prep = I => { if (!memo.has(I)) memo.set(I, prepare(I)); return memo.get(I); }; const emaMemo = new WeakMap(); function emaOf(I, p) { // EMA بنفس طريقة الأصل (أول قيمة = متوسط أول p إغلاق) if (!emaMemo.has(I)) emaMemo.set(I, {}); const mm = emaMemo.get(I); if (mm[p]) return mm[p]; const out = new Array(I.n).fill(null); let e = null; for (let k = p - 1; k < I.n; k++) { if (e === null) { e = 0; for (let j = 0; j < p; j++) e += I.C[j]; e /= p; } else e = I.C[k]*(2/(p+1)) + e*((p-1)/(p+1)); out[k] = e; } return (mm[p] = out); } const okTrend = (P, I, k, side, f) => { // f: قائمة فترات EMA؛ يكفي وحدة منهم توافق (افتراضي [200]) if (!f) return P.ema[k] !== null && (I.C[k] - P.ema[k])*side > 0; if (f.all) return f.all.every(p => { const e = emaOf(I, p)[k]; return e !== null && (I.C[k] - e)*side > 0; }); return f.some(p => { const e = emaOf(I, p)[k]; return e !== null && (I.C[k] - e)*side > 0; }); }; const hitsAt = (P, I, k, v2, f) => (P.events.get(k) || []).filter(x => !v2 || okTrend(P, I, k, x.side, f)); // للمراقبة الحية: نفس منطق prepare، بس بيرجّع السحب المعلّق (لسا ما صار كسر هيكل) لحد الشمعة kLast (مسكّرة) function watch(I, kLast) { const P = prep(I), out = {}; for (const side of [1, -1]) { let level = null, pending = null; for (let k = 0; k <= kLast; k++) { if (level !== null && P.atr[k] !== null && k >= 3) { const sweep = side === 1 ? (level - I.L[k] >= .1*P.atr[k] && I.C[k] > level) : (I.H[k] - level >= .1*P.atr[k] && I.C[k] < level); if (sweep) { const th = side === 1 ? Math.max(I.H[k-3], I.H[k-2], I.H[k-1]) : Math.min(I.L[k-3], I.L[k-2], I.L[k-1]); pending = [k, th, level]; } else if (pending && (I.C[k] - pending[1])*side > 0 && k - pending[0] >= 1 && k - pending[0] <= 5) pending = null; } if (pending && k - pending[0] > 5) pending = null; const i = k - 3; if (i >= 3) { const arr = side === 1 ? I.L : I.H; let ok = true; for (let j = i - 3; j <= k; j++) if (j !== i && !((arr[j] - arr[i])*side > 0)) { ok = false; break; } if (ok) level = arr[i]; } } // السحب لسا صالح للشمعة الجاية kLast+1 (لازم يكون الكسر خلال 1..5 شموع من السحب) if (pending && kLast + 1 - pending[0] <= 5) out[side] = { s: pending[0], th: pending[1], level: pending[2], base: side === 1 ? I.L[pending[0]] : I.H[pending[0]], left: 5 - (kLast + 1 - pending[0]) + 1 }; } return { pend: out, atr: P.atr[kLast], ema: P.ema[kLast] }; } const sig = v2 => (I, i) => { const h = hitsAt(prep(I), I, i, v2); return h.length ? h[0].side : 0; }; // آخر تمويل معروف لحد وقت ts (D.fund من المختبر: [{t, rate}] مرتبة) function fundAt(D, ts) { const F = D.fund; if (!F || !F.length) return null; let lo = 0, hi = F.length - 1, r = -1; while (lo <= hi) { const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1; if (F[mid].t <= ts) { r = mid; lo = mid + 1; } else hi = mid - 1; } return r < 0 || ts - F[r].t > 864e5 ? null : F[r].rate; } // تمويل قديم (أكتر من يوم) = ما في معلومة const fundOk = (sp, r, side) => { if (r === null || !sp.fundF) return true; const f = sp.fundF; if (f.mode === "crowd") return side === 1 ? r < f.x : r > -f.x; // تجنّب الجهة المزدحمة لما التمويل متطرف return side === 1 ? r <= f.base : r >= 0; }; // "strict": لونج بس لما التمويل مش فوق الأساس، شورت بس لما مش سالب function run(D, inds, sp, startTs) { const m = sp.mult, I = inds[m], P = prep(I), out = [], n = I.n; let pos = null, pend = null, busy = -1; for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) { let closedHere = false; if (pend) { const [hit, ks] = pend, sign = hit.side, stop = sp.sb === undefined ? hit.stopLoss : hit.base - sign*sp.sb*hit.atrk; let raw = I.O[k]; if (sp.limitPull) { // أمر ليمت بسعر أحسن: إغلاق الإشارة مسحوب باتجاه الوقف بنسبة limitPull من المسافة، صالح لشمعة وحدة const lvl = I.C[ks] - sign*sp.limitPull*Math.abs(I.C[ks] - stop); if (sign*(I.O[k] - lvl) <= 0) raw = I.O[k]; else if (sign === 1 ? I.L[k] <= lvl : I.H[k] >= lvl) raw = lvl; else raw = null; } if (raw === null) { pend = null; } else { if ((raw - stop)*sign > 0 && stop > 0 && !(sp.maxRisk && Math.abs(raw - stop)/raw > sp.maxRisk)) pos = { d: sign, e: k, entry: raw, stop, stop0: stop, target: sp.tpPct ? raw*(1 + sign*sp.tpPct) : raw + sign*(sp.R || 2)*Math.abs(raw - stop), score: sp.rankBy === "depth" ? -(hit.depth || 0) : Math.abs(raw - stop)/raw, ...(sp.riskPct ? { w: Math.min(sp.wMax || 3, sp.riskPct/(100*Math.abs(raw - stop)/raw)) } : {}) }; pend = null; } } if (pos) { const s = pos.d, o = I.O[k]; let raw = null, why = null; if (s*(o - pos.stop) <= 0) { raw = o; why = "INIT_STOP"; } else if (s === 1 ? I.L[k] <= pos.stop : I.H[k] >= pos.stop) { raw = pos.stop; why = "INIT_STOP"; } if (raw === null && (sp.partial || sp.partialPct) && !pos.half) { // جني نص الصفقة على partial×R أو partialPct% (أمر ليمت = Maker) const p1 = sp.partialPct ? pos.entry*(1 + s*sp.partialPct) : pos.entry + s*sp.partial*Math.abs(pos.entry - pos.stop0); let px1 = null; if (s*(o - p1) >= 0) px1 = o; else if (s === 1 ? I.H[k] >= p1 : I.L[k] <= p1) px1 = p1; if (px1 !== null) { pos.half = true; out.push({ name: sp.name, d: s, e: pos.e*m, x: k*m + m - 1, rawIn: pos.entry, rawOut: px1, why: "TARGET", open: false, stop: pos.stop, target: p1, w: (pos.w || 1)/2, score: pos.score, part: true }); if (sp.partialBE) pos.beNext = true; } } if (raw === null) { if (s*(o - pos.target) >= 0) { raw = o; why = "TARGET"; } else if (s === 1 ? I.H[k] >= pos.target : I.L[k] <= pos.target) { raw = pos.target; why = "TARGET"; } else if (k - pos.e + 1 >= (sp.hold || 20)) { raw = I.C[k]; why = "TIME"; } } if (raw !== null) { if (why === "INIT_STOP" && pos.moved) why = "TRAIL"; const wq = pos.half ? (pos.w || 1)/2 : pos.w; out.push({ name: sp.name, d: s, e: pos.e*m, x: k*m + m - 1, rawIn: pos.entry, rawOut: raw, why, open: false, stop: pos.stop, target: pos.target, score: pos.score, ...(wq ? { w: wq } : {}) }); pos = null; busy = k; closedHere = true; } else if (pos.beNext) { pos.beNext = false; const ns = s === 1 ? Math.max(pos.stop, pos.entry) : Math.min(pos.stop, pos.entry); if (ns !== pos.stop) { pos.stop = ns; pos.moved = true; } } else if (sp.be || sp.trailAtr) { // تعديل الوقف بعد إغلاق الشمعة (بيطبق من الشمعة الجاية) const r0 = Math.abs(pos.entry - pos.stop0), fav = s === 1 ? I.H[k] : I.L[k]; pos.best = pos.best === undefined ? fav : (s === 1 ? Math.max(pos.best, fav) : Math.min(pos.best, fav)); const gain = s*(pos.best - pos.entry); let ns = pos.stop; if (sp.be && gain >= sp.be*r0) ns = s === 1 ? Math.max(ns, pos.entry) : Math.min(ns, pos.entry); if (sp.trailAtr && gain >= (sp.trailFrom || 1)*r0 && P.atr[k] !== null) { const t = pos.best - s*sp.trailAtr*P.atr[k]; ns = s === 1 ? Math.max(ns, t) : Math.min(ns, t); } if (ns !== pos.stop) { pos.stop = ns; pos.moved = true; } } } let h = hitsAt(P, I, k, sp.v2, sp.emaF); if (h.length && sp.btcF) h = h.filter(x => btcTrend(D.t0 + (k+1)*m*900000, sp.btcF) === x.side); if (h.length && sp.coinEma) { const e = emaOf(I, sp.coinEma)[k]; if (e !== null) h = h.filter(x => (I.C[k] - e)*x.side > 0); } // اتجاه العملة نفسها (لما المتوسط لسا مش جاهز = بدون فلتر) if (h.length && sp.fundF) { const r = fundAt(D, D.t0 + (k+1)*m*900000); h = h.filter(x => fundOk(sp, r, x.side)); } if (h.length && sp.gannF && m === 16) h = h.filter(x => GDEC.near(I, k, x.side, sp.gannF)); if (h.length && sp.fibT && m === 16) { const w = fibTimeWin(I, k, sp.fibT.set || "A"); if (sp.fibT.inside ? !w : w) h = []; } if (!h.length) continue; const blocked = pos !== null || closedHere || k <= busy || k === n - 1 || D.t0 + (k+1)*m*900000 <= startTs || (k+1)*m >= D.o.length; if (!blocked) pend = [h[0], k]; } if (pos) { const wq = pos.half ? (pos.w || 1)/2 : pos.w; out.push({ name: sp.name, d: pos.d, e: pos.e*m, x: D.o.length - 1, rawIn: pos.entry, rawOut: D.c[D.c.length-1], why: "TIME", open: true, stop: pos.stop, target: pos.target, ...(wq ? { w: wq } : {}) }); } return out; } const mk = (label, mult, v2) => ({ name: "S15 Sweep+MSS " + label, mult, v2, sig: sig(v2), run, sm: 0, tm: 0, hold: 20, trail: null, cd: null, entry: "imm", src: "Replit #15" }); return { prepare, run, watch, strats: [mk("V2 15m", 1, true), mk("V2 1h", 4, true), mk("V2 4h", 16, true), mk("V1 15m", 1, false)] }; })(); // Replit #13 Ichimoku Cloud Trend (15m + 1h) and #8 LuxAlgo-style Trendline + TRAMA + Chandelier (15m + 4h). // Port of extended_rules.py / extended_indicators.py / backtesting.replay (300-bar rolling windows, next-open entry). const SEXT = (() => { const M15 = 900000; function agg(D, m) { const n = Math.floor(D.o.length / m), O = [], H = [], L = [], C = []; for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) { let hi = -Infinity, lo = Infinity; for (let j = k*m; j < (k+1)*m; j++) { if (D.h[j] > hi) hi = D.h[j]; if (D.l[j] < lo) lo = D.l[j]; } O.push(D.o[k*m]); H.push(hi); L.push(lo); C.push(D.c[(k+1)*m - 1]); } return { O, H, L, C, n }; } function ichi(B) { const n = B.n, mid = (i, p) => { if (i+1 < p) return null; let hi = -Infinity, lo = Infinity; for (let j = i-p+1; j <= i; j++) { if (B.H[j] > hi) hi = B.H[j]; if (B.L[j] < lo) lo = B.L[j]; } return (hi + lo) / 2; }; const t = [], k = [], pa = [], pb = []; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { t.push(mid(i, 9)); k.push(mid(i, 26)); pb.push(mid(i, 52)); pa.push(t[i] !== null && k[i] !== null ? (t[i] + k[i]) / 2 : null); } const ca = i => i >= 26 ? pa[i-26] : null, cb = i => i >= 26 ? pb[i-26] : null; return { t, k, ca, cb }; } // analyze()-style Wilder ATR14 / RSI14 on the window [s..e] function winAtrRsi(B, s, e) { let a = null, cnt = 0, sum = 0; for (let i = s+1; i <= e; i++) { const tr = Math.max(B.H[i] - B.L[i], Math.abs(B.H[i] - B.C[i-1]), Math.abs(B.L[i] - B.C[i-1])); if (a === null) { sum += tr; cnt++; if (cnt === 14) a = sum / 14; } else a = (a*13 + tr) / 14; } let g = null, l = null, gs = 0, ls = 0; cnt = 0; for (let i = s+1; i <= e; i++) { const d = B.C[i] - B.C[i-1], up = Math.max(d, 0), dn = Math.max(-d, 0); if (g === null) { gs += up; ls += dn; cnt++; if (cnt === 14) { g = gs / 14; l = ls / 14; } } else { g = (g*13 + up) / 14; l = (l*13 + dn) / 14; } } const r = g === null ? null : (g === 0 && l === 0 ? 50 : l === 0 ? 100 : 100 - 100 / (1 + g / l)); return { atr: a, rsi: r }; } const HLW = new WeakMap(); // 18/11 (أسرع، نفس النتيجة): أعلى/أدنى آخر period شمعة محسوبين مرة وحدة function hlWin(B, period) { let M = HLW.get(B.H); if (!M) { M = {}; HLW.set(B.H, M); } if (!M[period]) { const n = B.H.length, hi = new Float64Array(n), lo = new Float64Array(n); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { let h = -Infinity, l = Infinity; for (let j = i-period+1; j <= i; j++) { if (B.H[j] > h) h = B.H[j]; if (B.L[j] < l) l = B.L[j]; } hi[i] = h; lo[i] = l; } M[period] = { hi, lo }; } return M[period]; } function chandelierLast(B, s, e, period = 22, mult = 3) { let a = null, cnt = 0, sum = 0, ls = null, ss = null, dir = 1, last = null; const W = hlWin(B, period); for (let i = s; i <= e; i++) { if (i > s) { const tr = Math.max(B.H[i] - B.L[i], Math.abs(B.H[i] - B.C[i-1]), Math.abs(B.L[i] - B.C[i-1])); if (a === null) { sum += tr; cnt++; if (cnt === period) a = sum / period; } else a = (a*(period-1) + tr) / period; } if (a === null) { last = null; continue; } const hi = W.hi[i], lo = W.lo[i]; const rl = hi - mult*a, rs = lo + mult*a; if (ls !== null) { if (B.C[i] > ss) dir = 1; else if (B.C[i] < ls) dir = -1; ls = B.C[i-1] > ls ? Math.max(rl, ls) : rl; ss = B.C[i-1] < ss ? Math.min(rs, ss) : rs; } else { ls = rl; ss = rs; } last = true; } return last ? { dir, ls, ss } : null; // 18/11: كائن وحدة بالآخر بدل كائن لكل شمعة } function prepare(D) { const B = { O: D.o, H: D.h, L: D.l, C: D.c, n: D.o.length }; D = { o: B.O, h: B.H, l: B.L, c: B.C }; const H1 = agg(D, 4), H4 = agg(D, 16), n = B.n; const I15 = ichi(B), I1 = ichi(H1); // stochastic 14,3,3 (local) const raw = new Array(n).fill(null), sk = new Array(n).fill(null), sd = new Array(n).fill(null); for (let i = 13; i < n; i++) { let hi = -Infinity, lo = Infinity; for (let j = i-13; j <= i; j++) { if (B.H[j] > hi) hi = B.H[j]; if (B.L[j] < lo) lo = B.L[j]; } raw[i] = hi > lo ? 100*(B.C[i] - lo)/(hi - lo) : 50; } for (let i = 15; i < n; i++) sk[i] = (raw[i] + raw[i-1] + raw[i-2]) / 3; for (let i = 17; i < n; i++) sd[i] = (sk[i] + sk[i-1] + sk[i-2]) / 3; // TRAMA new-extreme flags (absolute; valid inside window when window index >= 99) const flag = new Array(n).fill(0); for (let i = 99; i < n; i++) { let hi = -Infinity, lo = Infinity; for (let j = i-99; j < i; j++) { if (B.H[j] > hi) hi = B.H[j]; if (B.L[j] < lo) lo = B.L[j]; } flag[i] = (B.H[i] > hi || B.L[i] < lo) ? 1 : 0; } const fcs = [0]; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) fcs.push(fcs[i] + flag[i]); // confirmed pivots width 2 (absolute) const ph = [], pl = []; for (let i = 2; i < n-2; i++) { let up = true, dn = true; for (const j of [i-2, i-1, i+1, i+2]) { if (!(B.H[i] > B.H[j])) up = false; if (!(B.L[i] < B.L[j])) dn = false; } if (up) ph.push(i); if (dn) pl.push(i); } const chMemo = new Map(); const ch4 = N4 => { if (!chMemo.has(N4)) chMemo.set(N4, N4 >= 200 ? chandelierLast(H4, Math.max(0, N4-300), N4-1) : null); return chMemo.get(N4); }; return { B, H1, H4, I15, I1, sk, sd, flag, fcs, ph, pl, ch4 }; } function trama(P, s, e) { const L = e - s + 1; if (L - 1 < 197) return null; const B = P.B; let v = 0; for (let j = s; j < s+99; j++) v += B.C[j]; v /= 99; for (let i = 197; i < L; i++) { const a = s+i; const m = (P.fcs[a+1] - P.fcs[a-98]) / 99; v += m*m*(B.C[a] - v); } return v; } function lastTwo(piv, s, k) { // pivots with window index>=2 and confirmed before bar k opens let lo = 0, hi = piv.length - 1, b = -1; while (lo <= hi) { const md = (lo + hi) >> 1; if (piv[md] <= k-3) { b = md; lo = md + 1; } else hi = md - 1; } // 18/11: بحث ثنائي (نفس النتيجة، الـpivots مرتبة) if (b < 1 || piv[b-1] < s+2) return null; return [piv[b-1], piv[b]]; } function lineBreaks(P, s, k) { const B = P.B, out = { up: false, down: false }; const r = lastTwo(P.ph, s, k); if (r) { const [a, b] = r; if (-(B.H[b] - B.H[a]) > 0) { const sl = (B.H[b] - B.H[a]) / (b - a); out.up = B.C[k-1] <= B.H[b] + sl*(k-1-b) && B.C[k] > B.H[b] + sl*(k-b); } } const u = lastTwo(P.pl, s, k); if (u) { const [a, b] = u; if (B.L[b] - B.L[a] > 0) { const sl = (B.L[b] - B.L[a]) / (b - a); out.down = B.C[k-1] >= B.L[b] + sl*(k-1-b) && B.C[k] < B.L[b] + sl*(k-b); } } return out; } function decide(P, id, k) { const B = P.B; if (k < 200) return null; const s = Math.max(0, k-299); if (id === "ichi") { const h = Math.floor((k+1)/4) - 1; if (h + 1 < 200) return null; const I = P.I15, J = P.I1; const vals = [I.t[k], I.k[k], I.ca(k), I.cb(k), I.t[k-1], I.k[k-1], I.ca(k-1), I.cb(k-1), J.t[h], J.k[h], J.ca(h), J.cb(h)]; if (vals.some(v => v === null) || k < 26 || h < 26) return null; const c = B.C[k], hp = P.H1.C[h], hmax = Math.max(J.ca(h), J.cb(h)), hmin = Math.min(J.ca(h), J.cb(h)); const buy = I.t[k-1] <= I.k[k-1] && I.t[k] > I.k[k] && c > Math.max(I.ca(k), I.cb(k)) && I.ca(k) >= I.cb(k) && c > B.H[k-26] && hp > hmax && J.t[h] > J.k[h]; const sell = I.t[k-1] >= I.k[k-1] && I.t[k] < I.k[k] && c < Math.min(I.ca(k), I.cb(k)) && I.ca(k) <= I.cb(k) && c < B.L[k-26] && hp < hmin && J.t[h] < J.k[h]; if (buy === sell) return null; const ar = winAtrRsi(B, s, k); if (ar.atr === null || ar.rsi === null) return null; return { d: buy ? 1 : -1, atr: ar.atr }; } // lux const N4 = Math.floor((k+1)/16); if (N4 < 200) return null; const ch = P.ch4(N4); if (!ch) return null; if (P.sk[k] === null || P.sd[k] === null) return null; // 18/11 (أسرع، نفس النتيجة بالزبط): كسر خط الترند أول (رخيص ونادر)، وبعدين TRAMA (غالي) const ln = lineBreaks(P, s, k); const u = ln.up && P.sk[k] >= P.sd[k] && ch.dir === 1, w = ln.down && P.sk[k] <= P.sd[k] && ch.dir === -1; if (!u && !w) return null; const t = trama(P, s, k); if (t === null) return null; const c = B.C[k]; const pre1 = u && c > t, pre2 = w && c < t; if (!pre1 && !pre2) return null; const ar = winAtrRsi(B, s, k); if (ar.atr === null || ar.rsi === null) return null; const buy = pre1 && ar.rsi > 50, sell = pre2 && ar.rsi < 50; if (buy === sell) return null; return { d: buy ? 1 : -1, atr: ar.atr, N4 }; } const memo = new WeakMap(); const prep = D => { if (!memo.has(D)) memo.set(D, prepare(D)); return memo.get(D); }; function run(D, inds, sp, startTs) { const P = prep(D), B = P.B, n = B.n, out = [], id = sp.ext; let pos = null, pend = null, pendExit = false; for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) { const W = p => (p.half ? { w: 0.5 } : {}); // بعد جني النص: الباقي نص الحجم if (pos && pendExit) { const gap = pos.d*(B.O[k] - pos.stop) <= 0; out.push({ name: sp.name, d: pos.d, e: pos.e, x: k, rawIn: pos.entry, rawOut: B.O[k], why: gap ? "INIT_STOP" : "CHAND_EXIT", open: false, stop: pos.stop, target: NaN, ...W(pos) }); pos = null; } pendExit = false; if (pend) { const e = B.O[k], dist = 1.5*pend.atr, stop = e - pend.d*dist, target = e + pend.d*2*dist; if (Math.min(stop, target) > 0) pos = { d: pend.d, e: k, entry: e, stop, target, N4: pend.N4 }; pend = null; } if (pos) { const s = pos.d, o = B.O[k]; let raw = null, why = null; const tp = id === "lux" && sp.tpPct ? pos.entry*(1 + s*sp.tpPct) : null; // هدف ثابت % (تجربة) if (s*(o - pos.stop) <= 0) { raw = o; why = "INIT_STOP"; } else if (id === "ichi" && s*(o - pos.target) >= 0) { raw = pos.target; why = "TARGET"; } else if (tp !== null && k !== pos.e && s*(o - tp) >= 0) { raw = o; why = "TGT_GAP"; } else if (s === 1 ? B.L[k] <= pos.stop : B.H[k] >= pos.stop) { raw = pos.stop; why = "INIT_STOP"; } else if (id === "ichi" && (s === 1 ? B.H[k] >= pos.target : B.L[k] <= pos.target)) { raw = pos.target; why = "TARGET"; } else if (tp !== null && (s === 1 ? B.H[k] >= tp : B.L[k] <= tp)) { raw = tp; why = "TARGET"; } if (raw === null && id === "lux" && sp.partialPct && !pos.half) { // جني نص الصفقة على partialPct (ليمت = Maker) const p1 = pos.entry*(1 + s*sp.partialPct); if (s === 1 ? B.H[k] >= p1 : B.L[k] <= p1) { pos.half = true; out.push({ name: sp.name, d: s, e: pos.e, x: k, rawIn: pos.entry, rawOut: p1, why: "TARGET", open: false, stop: pos.stop, target: p1, w: 0.5, part: true }); if (sp.partialBE) pos.beNext = true; } } if (raw !== null) { out.push({ name: sp.name, d: s, e: pos.e, x: k, rawIn: pos.entry, rawOut: raw, why, open: false, stop: pos.stop, target: id === "ichi" ? pos.target : NaN, ...W(pos) }); pos = null; } else { if (pos.beNext) { pos.beNext = false; pos.stop = s === 1 ? Math.max(pos.stop, pos.entry) : Math.min(pos.stop, pos.entry); } if (id === "lux") { const N4 = Math.floor((k+1)/16); if (N4 > pos.N4) { const ch = P.ch4(N4); if (ch && ch.dir === -pos.d) pendExit = true; } } } } const sig = decide(P, id, k); if (!sig || pos || k === n-1 || D.t0 + (k+1)*M15 <= startTs) continue; if (id === "lux") sig.N4 = Math.floor((k+1)/16); pend = sig; } if (pos) out.push({ name: sp.name, d: pos.d, e: pos.e, x: n-1, rawIn: pos.entry, rawOut: B.C[n-1], why: "TIME", open: true, stop: pos.stop, target: id === "ichi" ? pos.target : NaN, ...(pos.half ? { w: 0.5 } : {}) }); return out; } const memoI = new WeakMap(); const sigOf = id => (I, i) => { if (!memoI.has(I)) memoI.set(I, prepare({ o: I.O, h: I.H, l: I.L, c: I.C })); const r = decide(memoI.get(I), id, i); return r ? r.d : 0; }; const mk = (name, ext) => ({ name, mult: 1, ext, sig: sigOf(ext), run, sm: 0, tm: 0, hold: 0, trail: null, cd: null, entry: "imm", src: ext === "ichi" ? "Replit #13" : "Replit #8" }); return { prepare, run, decide, strats: [mk("#13 Ichimoku 15m+1h", "ichi"), mk("#8 Lux Trendline+TRAMA+Chandelier", "lux")] }; })(); // Replit 5m scalping families: legacy #1-#7 (strategy_rules.rule_decision + score gate) and extended // #9-#12, #14 (extended_rules.evaluate_extended). Port of backtesting.replay on a 5m base: // 300-bar rolling windows, entry on next 5m open, ATR 1.5 stop / 2R target, stop-first protective exits. const S5 = (() => { const M5 = 300000, M15 = 900000, VER = 1; const LEG = { volumeRatio: 1.5, rsiLow: 30, rsiHigh: 70, levelToleranceAtr: 0.25, wickRatio: 0.4, stopAtr: 1.5, rewardRisk: 2, minScore: 50 }; const EXT = { ...LEG, volumeRatio: 1.2, minScore: 0 }; function series(O, H, L, C, V, t0, step) { const n = O.length, S = { O, H, L, C, V, t0, step, n }; const emaG = p => { const out = new Array(n).fill(null); if (n < p) return out; let v = 0; for (let i = 0; i < p; i++) v += C[i]; v /= p; out[p-1] = v; for (let i = p; i < n; i++) { v += (C[i] - v)*2/(p+1); out[i] = v; } return out; }; S.e20 = emaG(20); const e12 = emaG(12), e26 = emaG(26); // MACD signal = EMA9 over the macd values list (starts where EMA26 exists) S.hist = new Array(n).fill(null); { let sig = null, cnt = 0, sum = 0; for (let i = 25; i < n; i++) { const m = e12[i] - e26[i]; if (sig === null) { sum += m; cnt++; if (cnt === 9) sig = sum/9; } else sig += (m - sig)*2/10; if (sig !== null) S.hist[i] = m - sig; } } // RSI14 (Wilder seeded by mean) S.rsi = new Array(n).fill(null); { let g = null, l = null, gs = 0, ls = 0, cnt = 0; for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) { const d = C[i] - C[i-1], up = Math.max(d, 0), dn = Math.max(-d, 0); if (g === null) { gs += up; ls += dn; cnt++; if (cnt === 14) { g = gs/14; l = ls/14; } } else { g = (g*13 + up)/14; l = (l*13 + dn)/14; } if (g !== null) S.rsi[i] = g === 0 && l === 0 ? 50 : l === 0 ? 100 : 100 - 100/(1 + g/l); } } S.atr = new Array(n).fill(null); { let a = null, cnt = 0, sum = 0; for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) { const tr = Math.max(H[i] - L[i], Math.abs(H[i] - C[i-1]), Math.abs(L[i] - C[i-1])); if (a === null) { sum += tr; cnt++; if (cnt === 14) a = sum/14; } else a = (a*13 + tr)/14; S.atr[i] = a; } } S.cs = [0]; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) S.cs.push(S.cs[i] + C[i]); S.ph = []; S.pl = []; for (let i = 2; i < n-2; i++) { let up = true, dn = true; for (const j of [i-2, i-1, i+1, i+2]) { if (!(H[i] > H[j])) up = false; if (!(L[i] < L[j])) dn = false; } if (up) S.ph.push(i); if (dn) S.pl.push(i); } S.memo = new Map(); S.st = new Map(); return S; } const lastLE = (arr, x) => { let lo = 0, hi = arr.length - 1, r = -1; while (lo <= hi) { const m = (lo + hi) >> 1; if (arr[m] <= x) { r = m; lo = m + 1; } else hi = m - 1; } return r; }; // analyze() on the window ending at k (last 300 bars) function an(S, k) { if (S.memo.has(k)) return S.memo.get(k); const s = Math.max(0, k - 299), len = k - s + 1, C = S.C; const r = { ready: len >= 200 }; const emaW = p => { if (len < p) return null; let v = (S.cs[s+p] - S.cs[s])/p; for (let i = s+p; i <= k; i++) v += (C[i] - v)*2/(p+1); return v; }; r.ema20 = len >= 20 ? S.e20[k] : null; r.ema50 = emaW(50); r.ema200 = emaW(200); const rw = i => i - s >= 14 ? S.rsi[i] : null; r.rsi = rw(k); r.atr = k - s >= 14 ? S.atr[k] : null; r.hist = k - s >= 33 ? S.hist[k] : null; const st = i => { if (i - s < 27) return null; let lo = Infinity, hi = -Infinity; for (let j = i-13; j <= i; j++) { lo = Math.min(lo, S.rsi[j]); hi = Math.max(hi, S.rsi[j]); } return hi === lo ? 50 : (S.rsi[i] - lo)/(hi - lo)*100; }; const K = i => { if (i - s < 29) return null; return (st(i) + st(i-1) + st(i-2))/3; }; r.sk = K(k); r.sd = k - s >= 31 ? (K(k) + K(k-1) + K(k-2))/3 : null; if (len >= 20) { let m = 0; for (let j = k-19; j <= k; j++) m += C[j]; m /= 20; let v = 0; for (let j = k-19; j <= k; j++) v += (C[j] - m)**2; const sd = Math.sqrt(v/20); r.bbU = m + 2*sd; r.bbL = m - 2*sd; } else { r.bbU = r.bbL = null; } r.trend = r.ema50 !== null && r.ema200 !== null ? (C[k] > r.ema50 && r.ema50 > r.ema200 ? "bullish" : C[k] < r.ema50 && r.ema50 < r.ema200 ? "bearish" : "mixed") : "unavailable"; // price action (pivots inside the window) const inW = (arr, maxI) => { const out = []; let q = lastLE(arr, maxI); while (q >= 0 && arr[q] >= s+2 && out.length < 2) { out.unshift(arr[q]); q--; } return out; }; const hh = inW(S.ph, k-2), ll = inW(S.pl, k-2); r.structure = "undetermined"; if (hh.length >= 2 && ll.length >= 2) { const a = S.H[hh[1]] > S.H[hh[0]], b = S.L[ll[1]] > S.L[ll[0]], c = S.H[hh[1]] < S.H[hh[0]], d = S.L[ll[1]] < S.L[ll[0]]; r.structure = a && b ? "higher_highs_higher_lows" : c && d ? "lower_highs_lower_lows" : "range"; } const rh = inW(S.ph, k-3), rl = inW(S.pl, k-3); r.resistance = rh.length ? S.H[rh[rh.length-1]] : null; r.support = rl.length ? S.L[rl[rl.length-1]] : null; r.breakout = "none"; if (len > 1) { const p = C[k-1], c = C[k]; if (r.resistance !== null && p <= r.resistance && r.resistance < c) r.breakout = "above_resistance"; else if (r.support !== null && p >= r.support && r.support > c) r.breakout = "below_support"; } r.vr = null; if (S.V && k >= 1) { let sum = 0, ok = true; for (let j = Math.max(0, k-20); j < k; j++) { if (S.V[j] == null) { ok = false; break; } sum += S.V[j]; } const avg = sum/20; if (ok && S.V[k] != null) r.vr = avg > 0 ? S.V[k]/avg : null; } if (S.memo.size > 300000) S.memo.clear(); S.memo.set(k, r); return r; } const fin = v => v !== null && v !== undefined && Number.isFinite(v); const KEYS = ["ema20", "ema50", "ema200", "rsi", "hist", "sk", "sd", "bbL", "bbU", "atr"]; // ---- windowed extended indicators (exact on the 300-bar window) ---- function rmaWin(vals, p) { const out = new Array(vals.length).fill(null); let v = null, seed = 0, cnt = 0; for (let i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) { const x = vals[i]; if (x === null) { v = null; seed = 0; cnt = 0; continue; } if (v === null) { seed += x; cnt++; if (cnt === p) v = seed/p; } else v = (v*(p-1) + x)/p; out[i] = v; } return out; } function atrWin(S, s, k, p) { const tr = [null]; for (let i = s+1; i <= k; i++) tr.push(Math.max(S.H[i] - S.L[i], Math.abs(S.H[i] - S.C[i-1]), Math.abs(S.L[i] - S.C[i-1]))); return rmaWin(tr, p); } function supertrendWin(S, k, p = 10, mult = 3) { // returns [prevDir, lastDir] on the window ending at k const s = Math.max(0, k - 299); let atr = null, sum = 0, cnt = 0, up = null, lo = null, dir = 1, prev = null, last = null; for (let i = s; i <= k; i++) { if (i > s) { const tr = Math.max(S.H[i] - S.L[i], Math.abs(S.H[i] - S.C[i-1]), Math.abs(S.L[i] - S.C[i-1])); if (atr === null) { sum += tr; cnt++; if (cnt === p) atr = sum/p; } else atr = (atr*(p-1) + tr)/p; } prev = last; if (atr === null) { last = null; continue; } const mid = (S.H[i] + S.L[i])/2; let u = mid + mult*atr, l = mid - mult*atr; if (up !== null) { u = (u < up || S.C[i-1] > up) ? u : up; l = (l > lo || S.C[i-1] < lo) ? l : lo; if (dir === -1 && S.C[i] > u) dir = 1; else if (dir === 1 && S.C[i] < l) dir = -1; } else dir = S.C[i] >= mid ? 1 : -1; up = u; lo = l; last = dir; } return [prev, last]; } function adxWin(S, k, p = 14) { const s = Math.max(0, k - 299), pos = [null], neg = [null]; for (let i = s+1; i <= k; i++) { const u = S.H[i] - S.H[i-1], d = S.L[i-1] - S.L[i]; pos.push(u > d && u > 0 ? u : 0); neg.push(d > u && d > 0 ? d : 0); } const atr = atrWin(S, s, k, p), P = rmaWin(pos, p), N = rmaWin(neg, p), dx = []; let pp = null, nn = null; for (let w = 0; w < atr.length; w++) { const a = atr[w]; if (a === null) { dx.push(null); pp = nn = null; continue; } pp = a ? 100*P[w]/a : 0; nn = a ? 100*N[w]/a : 0; dx.push(pp + nn ? 100*Math.abs(pp - nn)/(pp + nn) : 0); } const v = rmaWin(dx, p), last = v.length - 1; return v[last] === null ? null : { adx: v[last], plus: pp, minus: nn }; } function vwapAt(S, s, i) { // full-UTC-session VWAP, None if the session started before the window const day = Math.floor((S.t0 + i*S.step)/86400000)*86400000, first = Math.round((day - S.t0)/S.step); if (first < s || first < 0) return null; let vol = 0, val = 0; for (let j = first; j <= i; j++) { vol += S.V[j]; val += (S.H[j] + S.L[j] + S.C[j])/3*S.V[j]; } return vol > 0 ? val/vol : null; } function divergence(S, k, maxBars = 60) { const s = Math.max(0, k - 299), out = { bullish: false, bearish: false, bullishBreak: false, bearishBreak: false }; // RSI on the window (Wilder seeded inside the window) const R = new Array(k - s + 1).fill(null); { let g = null, l = null, gs = 0, ls = 0, cnt = 0; for (let w = 1; w <= k - s; w++) { const d = S.C[s+w] - S.C[s+w-1], up = Math.max(d, 0), dn = Math.max(-d, 0); if (g === null) { gs += up; ls += dn; cnt++; if (cnt === 14) { g = gs/14; l = ls/14; } } else { g = (g*13 + up)/14; l = (l*13 + dn)/14; } if (g !== null) R[w] = g === 0 && l === 0 ? 50 : l === 0 ? 100 : 100 - 100/(1 + g/l); } } const rv = i => R[i - s]; const pick = arr => { const o = []; for (let q = lastLE(arr, k-3); q >= 0 && arr[q] >= s+2; q--) o.unshift(arr[q]); return o; }; for (const [name, piv, opp, sign] of [["bullish", S.pl, S.ph, 1], ["bearish", S.ph, S.pl, -1]]) { const el = pick(piv).filter(i => rv(i) !== null); if (el.length < 2) continue; const a = el[el.length-2], b = el[el.length-1], P = sign === 1 ? S.L : S.H; out[name] = (k - b <= maxBars) && sign*(P[b] - P[a]) < 0 && sign*(rv(b) - rv(a)) > 0; const btw = pick(opp).filter(i => a < i && i < b); if (btw.length) { const Q = sign === 1 ? S.H : S.L, lvl = sign === 1 ? Math.max(...btw.map(i => Q[i])) : Math.min(...btw.map(i => Q[i])); out[name + "Break"] = out[name] && (sign === 1 ? S.C[k-1] <= lvl && lvl < S.C[k] : S.C[k-1] >= lvl && lvl > S.C[k]); } } return out; } // ---- decisions ---- function legacy(id, S, k, hS, hk) { const i = an(S, k), p = an(S, k-1), c = { o: S.O[k], h: S.H[k], l: S.L[k], c: S.C[k] }, pc = S.C[k-1], po = S.O[k-1]; if (!i.ready || !p.ready) return null; if (KEYS.some(x => !fin(i[x]) || !fin(p[x]))) return null; const bull = c.c > c.o, bear = c.c < c.o, range = c.h - c.l, body = Math.abs(c.c - c.o), validBody = range > 0 && body/range >= 0.4; let long = false, short = false; if (id === "ema") { const vok = i.vr !== null && i.vr >= 1.2; long = p.ema20 <= p.ema50 && i.ema20 > i.ema50 && c.c > i.ema200 && 50 <= i.rsi && i.rsi <= LEG.rsiHigh && bull && vok; short = p.ema20 >= p.ema50 && i.ema20 < i.ema50 && c.c < i.ema200 && LEG.rsiLow <= i.rsi && i.rsi <= 50 && bear && vok; } else if (id === "rsi") { long = p.rsi < LEG.rsiLow && LEG.rsiLow <= i.rsi && bull && validBody; short = p.rsi > LEG.rsiHigh && LEG.rsiHigh >= i.rsi && bear && validBody; } else if (id === "macd") { long = p.hist <= 0 && 0 < i.hist && c.c > i.ema50 && bull; short = p.hist >= 0 && 0 > i.hist && c.c < i.ema50 && bear; } else if (id === "bb") { long = pc < p.bbL && c.c >= i.bbL && bull && validBody; short = pc > p.bbU && c.c <= i.bbU && bear && validBody; } else if (id === "brk") { const vr = i.vr !== null && i.vr >= LEG.volumeRatio, sb = range > 0 && body/range >= 0.5; long = i.breakout === "above_resistance" && vr && sb && c.c > i.ema200; short = i.breakout === "below_support" && vr && sb && c.c < i.ema200; } else if (id === "sr") { const tol = i.atr*LEG.levelToleranceAtr, lw = Math.min(c.o, c.c) - c.l, uw = c.h - Math.max(c.o, c.c); long = i.support !== null && Math.abs(c.l - i.support) <= tol && c.c > i.support && bull && range > 0 && lw/range >= LEG.wickRatio; short = i.resistance !== null && Math.abs(c.h - i.resistance) <= tol && c.c < i.resistance && bear && range > 0 && uw/range >= LEG.wickRatio; } else if (id === "mtf") { if (hk < 0) return null; const h = an(hS, hk); if (!h.ready || !fin(h.rsi) || !fin(h.hist)) return null; const eb = bull && pc < po && c.c > po && c.o <= pc, es = bear && pc > po && c.c < po && c.o >= pc; long = h.trend === "bullish" && h.rsi > 50 && h.hist > 0 && h.structure === "higher_highs_higher_lows" && i.rsi > 50 && i.hist > 0 && p.sk <= p.sd && i.sk > i.sd && (i.breakout === "above_resistance" || eb); short = h.trend === "bearish" && h.rsi < 50 && h.hist < 0 && h.structure === "lower_highs_lower_lows" && i.rsi < 50 && i.hist < 0 && p.sk >= p.sd && i.sk < i.sd && (i.breakout === "below_support" || es); } if (long === short) return null; return score(long ? 1 : -1, i, S, k, LEG); } function score(d, i, S, k, R) { const pos = d === 1; const checks = [i.trend === (pos ? "bullish" : "bearish"), pos ? i.rsi > 50 && i.hist > 0 : i.rsi < 50 && i.hist < 0, i.structure === (pos ? "higher_highs_higher_lows" : "lower_highs_lower_lows"), i.vr !== null && i.vr >= R.volumeRatio]; const sc = Math.round(checks.filter(Boolean).length/4*100*1e4)/1e4, dist = i.atr*R.stopAtr; const stop = S.C[k] - d*dist, target = S.C[k] + d*dist*R.rewardRisk; if (sc < R.minScore || !(dist > 0) || !Number.isFinite(stop) || !Number.isFinite(target) || Math.min(stop, target) <= 0) return null; return { d, atr: i.atr, score: sc }; } function extended(id, S, k, hS, hk) { const i = an(S, k), p = an(S, k-1); if (!i.ready || !p.ready) return null; if (hk < 0) return null; const h = an(hS, hk); if (!h.ready || hk + 1 < 200) return null; if (!fin(i.atr) || !fin(i.rsi)) return null; const o = S.O[k], hi = S.H[k], lo = S.L[k], c = S.C[k], bull = c > o, bear = c < o, atr = i.atr, body = Math.abs(c - o), width = hi - lo; const volume = i.vr !== null && i.vr >= EXT.volumeRatio, tu = h.trend === "bullish", td = h.trend === "bearish"; const s = Math.max(0, k - 299); let B, Sx; if (id === "vwap") { const v1 = vwapAt(S, s, k), v2 = vwapAt(S, s, k-1); if (v1 === null || v2 === null || i.vr === null) return null; const sep = h.atr ? Math.abs(h.ema20 - h.ema50)/h.atr : 0; const q = sep >= 0.1 && body >= atr*0.1 && atr > 0; B = [tu, S.C[k-1] <= v2, c > v1 && bull, volume, q]; Sx = [td, S.C[k-1] >= v2, c < v1 && bear, volume, q]; } else if (id === "st") { const st = supertrendWin(S, k); if (!hS.stm) hS.stm = new Map(); if (!hS.stm.has(hk)) hS.stm.set(hk, supertrendWin(hS, hk)); const hst = hS.stm.get(hk); const s1 = st[1], s2 = st[0], hs = hst[1]; if (s1 == null || s2 == null || hs == null) return null; if (!(s2 === -1 && s1 === 1) && !(s2 === 1 && s1 === -1)) return null; // no flip: neither side can pass const a = adxWin(S, k); if (!a) return null; const strong = a.adx >= 20, bounded = atr > 0 && width <= 2.5*atr; B = [s2 === -1 && s1 === 1, hs === 1, strong, a.plus > a.minus, bounded]; Sx = [s2 === 1 && s1 === -1, hs === -1, strong, a.minus > a.plus, bounded]; } else if (id === "sweep") { const hs0 = Math.max(0, hk - 299), openT = S.t0 + k*S.step; // confirmed 15m pivots inside the 15m window, confirmed before this 5m bar opened const conf = (arr) => { for (let q = lastLE(arr, hk - 2); q >= 0 && arr[q] >= hs0 + 2; q--) { const j = arr[q]; if (hS.t0 + (j + 3)*hS.step - 1 < openT) return j; } return -1; }; const sj = conf(hS.pl), rj = conf(hS.ph), sup = sj >= 0 ? hS.L[sj] : null, res = rj >= 0 ? hS.H[rj] : null; B = [sup !== null, sup !== null && lo < sup, sup !== null && c > sup, bull, width > 0 && (Math.min(o, c) - lo)/width >= EXT.wickRatio]; Sx = [res !== null, res !== null && hi > res, res !== null && c < res, bear, width > 0 && (hi - Math.max(o, c))/width >= EXT.wickRatio]; } else if (id === "don") { if (k - s < 20 || i.vr === null) return null; let ch = -Infinity, cl = Infinity; for (let j = k-20; j < k; j++) { ch = Math.max(ch, S.H[j]); cl = Math.min(cl, S.L[j]); } B = [S.C[k-1] <= ch && ch < c, tu, volume, bull && body >= atr*0.1 && width > 0 && (hi - c)/width <= .25]; Sx = [S.C[k-1] >= cl && cl > c, td, volume, bear && body >= atr*0.1 && width > 0 && (c - lo)/width <= .25]; } else if (id === "div") { const dv = divergence(S, k); B = [dv.bullish, dv.bullishBreak, bull, h.structure !== "lower_highs_lower_lows"]; Sx = [dv.bearish, dv.bearishBreak, bear, h.structure !== "higher_highs_higher_lows"]; } const L = B.every(Boolean), Sh = Sx.every(Boolean); if (L === Sh) return null; return score(L ? 1 : -1, i, S, k, EXT); } // ---- data ---- function aggS(O, H, L, C, V, t0, m, step) { const n = Math.floor(O.length/m), o = [], h = [], l = [], c = [], v = V ? [] : null; for (let q = 0; q < n; q++) { let hi = -Infinity, lo = Infinity, vol = 0, ok = true; for (let j = q*m; j < (q+1)*m; j++) { hi = Math.max(hi, H[j]); lo = Math.min(lo, L[j]); if (V) { if (V[j] == null) ok = false; else vol += V[j]; } } o.push(O[q*m]); h.push(hi); l.push(lo); c.push(C[(q+1)*m - 1]); if (v) v.push(ok ? vol : null); } return series(o, h, l, c, v, t0, step*m); } function prepare(D) { const m5 = D.m5, S5 = series(m5.o, m5.h, m5.l, m5.c, m5.v, m5.t0, M5); // 15m volume from the 5m bars (only where the 5m data covers the whole 15m bar) const v15 = new Array(D.o.length).fill(null); for (let q = 0; q < D.o.length; q++) { const a = Math.round((D.t0 + q*M15 - m5.t0)/M5); if (a >= 0 && a + 2 < m5.o.length) v15[q] = m5.v[a] + m5.v[a+1] + m5.v[a+2]; } const S15 = series(D.o, D.h, D.l, D.c, v15, D.t0, M15); const S1h = aggS(D.o, D.h, D.l, D.c, null, D.t0, 4, M15), S4h = aggS(D.o, D.h, D.l, D.c, null, D.t0, 16, M15); return { S5, S15, S1h, S4h }; } const memo = new WeakMap(); const closedIdx = (S, cutoff) => Math.floor((cutoff + 1 - S.t0)/S.step) - 1; // last bar with closeTime <= cutoff function run(D, inds, sp, startTs) { if (!D.m5 || D.m5.o.length < 300) return []; if (!memo.has(D)) memo.set(D, prepare(D)); const P = memo.get(D), B = P.S5, n = B.n, out = [], R = sp.legacy ? LEG : EXT; const HT = { "15m": P.S15, "1h": P.S1h, "4h": P.S4h }; const modes = sp.id === "mtf" ? [["15m", "4h"], ["5m", "1h"]] : [[sp.tf || "5m", sp.htf || null]]; const toE = ts => (ts - D.t0)/M15; let pos = null, pend = null, j0 = 0; // استكمال من آخر نقطة محفوظة (نفس النتيجة بالضبط، بس أسرع) const store = D.s5ck, ck = store && store[sp.name]; if (ck && ck.ver === VER && ck.t0 === B.t0 && ck.d0 === D.t0 && ck.startTs === startTs && ck.j < n && ck.j >= 200 && ck.c === B.C[ck.j] && ck.o === B.O[ck.j]) { for (const t of ck.out) out.push(t); pos = ck.pos && { ...ck.pos }; pend = ck.pend && { ...ck.pend }; j0 = ck.j; } for (let j = j0; j < n; j++) { if (store && j === n - 1) store[sp.name] = { ver: VER, t0: B.t0, d0: D.t0, startTs, j, c: B.C[j], o: B.O[j], out: out.slice(), pos: pos && { ...pos }, pend: pend && { ...pend } }; if (pend) { const e = B.O[j], dist = R.stopAtr*pend.atr, stop = e - pend.d*dist, target = e + pend.d*dist*R.rewardRisk; if (Math.min(stop, target) > 0) pos = { d: pend.d, j, entry: e, stop, target }; pend = null; } if (pos) { const s = pos.d, o = B.O[j]; let raw = null, why = null; if (s*(o - pos.stop) <= 0) { raw = o; why = "INIT_STOP"; } else if (s*(o - pos.target) >= 0) { raw = pos.target; why = "TARGET"; } else if (s === 1 ? B.L[j] <= pos.stop : B.H[j] >= pos.stop) { raw = pos.stop; why = "INIT_STOP"; } else if (s === 1 ? B.H[j] >= pos.target : B.L[j] <= pos.target) { raw = pos.target; why = "TARGET"; } if (raw !== null) { out.push({ name: sp.name, d: s, e: toE(B.t0 + pos.j*M5), x: toE(B.t0 + (j+1)*M5) - 1, rawIn: pos.entry, rawOut: raw, why, open: false, stop: pos.stop, target: pos.target }); pos = null; } } if (j < 200) continue; const cutoff = B.t0 + (j+1)*M5 - 1; const cands = []; for (const [tf, htf] of modes) { const S = tf === "5m" ? B : HT[tf], k = closedIdx(S, cutoff); if (k < 1 || S.t0 + (k+1)*S.step - 1 !== cutoff) continue; const hS = htf ? HT[htf] : null, hk = hS ? closedIdx(hS, cutoff) : -1; const r = sp.legacy ? legacy(sp.id, S, k, hS, hk) : extended(sp.id, S, k, hS, hk); if (r) cands.push({ ...r, mode: (htf || "") + "/" + tf }); } if (!cands.length) continue; cands.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || (a.mode < b.mode ? -1 : a.mode > b.mode ? 1 : 0)); if (pos || j === n - 1 || B.t0 + (j+1)*M5 <= startTs) continue; pend = cands[0]; } if (pos) out.push({ name: sp.name, d: pos.d, e: toE(B.t0 + pos.j*M5), x: D.o.length - 1, rawIn: pos.entry, rawOut: B.C[n-1], why: "TIME", open: true, stop: pos.stop, target: pos.target }); return out; } const mk = (name, id, legacy, src, tf = "5m", htf = null) => ({ name, id, legacy, tf, htf, mult: 0, sig: () => 0, run, sm: 0, tm: 0, hold: 0, trail: null, cd: null, entry: "imm", src, m5: true }); return { run, an, series, prepare, strats: [ mk("#1 EMA Trend Scalping 5m", "ema", true, "Replit #1"), mk("#2 RSI Mean Reversion 5m", "rsi", true, "Replit #2"), mk("#3 MACD Momentum 5m", "macd", true, "Replit #3"), mk("#4 Bollinger Reversion 5m", "bb", true, "Replit #4"), mk("#5 Breakout + Volume 5m", "brk", true, "Replit #5"), mk("#6 Support & Resistance 5m", "sr", true, "Replit #6"), mk("#7 MTF Triple Confirmation", "mtf", true, "Replit #7"), mk("#9 VWAP Pullback 5m", "vwap", false, "Replit #9", "5m", "15m"), mk("#10 Supertrend + ADX 5m", "st", false, "Replit #10", "5m", "15m"), mk("#11 Liquidity Sweep 5m", "sweep", false, "Replit #11", "5m", "15m"), mk("#12 Donchian Breakout 5m", "don", false, "Replit #12", "5m", "15m"), mk("#14 RSI Divergence 5m", "div", false, "Replit #14", "5m", "15m"), ] }; })(); // 1H structure + 5m supply/demand first-retest (port of strategy_1h_5m_supply_demand.generate_signal). // Caller windows (fixed here): last 300 closed 1H bars, last 1200 5m bars. Replay: entry on the next 5m open, // structural stop/target kept as levels, stop-first exits, one position at a time per coin. const SSD = (() => { const M5 = 300000, H1 = 3600000, M15 = 900000, VER = 1; const CFG = { left: 2, right: 2, atrP: 14, disp: 0.8, search: 6, maxAge: 72, sb: 0.20, rej: 0.25, R: 2.0, minR: 1.5, W1: 300, W5: 1200, confirmBars: 0 }; // pivots: left non-strict, right strict (rightmost of equal swings) function pivots(A, isHigh, left, right) { const out = []; for (let i = left; i < A.length - right; i++) { const v = A[i]; let ok = true; for (let j = i - left; j < i && ok; j++) ok = isHigh ? v >= A[j] : v <= A[j]; for (let j = i + 1; j <= i + right && ok; j++) ok = isHigh ? v > A[j] : v < A[j]; if (ok) out.push(i); } return out; } const lastLE = (arr, x) => { let lo = 0, hi = arr.length - 1, r = -1; while (lo <= hi) { const m = (lo + hi) >> 1; if (arr[m] <= x) { r = m; lo = m + 1; } else hi = m - 1; } return r; }; const lastTwo = (arr, lo, hi) => { const q = lastLE(arr, hi); return q >= 1 && arr[q-1] >= lo ? [arr[q-1], arr[q]] : null; }; function smaAtr(O, H, L, C, i, p) { if (i < p) return null; let s = 0; for (let k = i - p + 1; k <= i; k++) s += Math.max(H[k] - L[k], Math.abs(H[k] - C[k-1]), Math.abs(L[k] - C[k-1])); return s/p; } function prepare(D, cfg) { const m5 = D.m5, n5 = m5.o.length; // 1H bars from the 15m history (long history, hour-aligned) const first = Math.ceil(D.t0 / H1) * H1, a0 = Math.round((first - D.t0)/M15), n1 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((D.o.length - a0)/4)); const O = [], H = [], L = [], C = []; for (let q = 0; q < n1; q++) { let hi = -Infinity, lo = Infinity; for (let j = a0 + q*4; j < a0 + (q+1)*4; j++) { hi = Math.max(hi, D.h[j]); lo = Math.min(lo, D.l[j]); } O.push(D.o[a0 + q*4]); H.push(hi); L.push(lo); C.push(D.c[a0 + q*4 + 3]); } const P = { m5, n5, h1: { O, H, L, C, t0: first, n: n1 }, cfg, ph1: pivots(H, true, cfg.left, cfg.right), pl1: pivots(L, false, cfg.left, cfg.right), ph5: pivots(m5.h, true, cfg.left, cfg.right), pl5: pivots(m5.l, false, cfg.left, cfg.right), zmemo: new Map(), touch: new Map() }; return P; } // window [s..e] of 1H: trend + zones (as generate_signal sees them) function h1State(P, e) { if (P.zmemo.has(e)) return P.zmemo.get(e); const { O, H, L, C, t0 } = P.h1, cfg = P.cfg, s = Math.max(0, e - cfg.W1 + 1), len = e - s + 1; let res = null; if (len >= Math.max(60, cfg.atrP + cfg.left + cfg.right + 5)) { const ph = lastTwo(P.ph1, s + cfg.left, e - cfg.right), pl = lastTwo(P.pl1, s + cfg.left, e - cfg.right); let trend = "NEUTRAL"; if (ph && pl) { if (H[ph[1]] > H[ph[0]] && L[pl[1]] > L[pl[0]]) trend = "BULLISH"; else if (H[ph[1]] < H[ph[0]] && L[pl[1]] < L[pl[0]]) trend = "BEARISH"; } const zones = []; // only BOS bars young enough can survive the age filter const lastOpen = t0 + e*H1, minIdx = Math.max(s + cfg.atrP + 1, s + 1); for (let i = Math.max(minIdx, e - cfg.maxAge - 2); i <= e; i++) { const atr = smaAtr(O, H, L, C, i, cfg.atrP); if (atr === null || i - s < cfg.atrP || !(atr > 0)) continue; if (Math.abs(C[i] - O[i]) < cfg.disp*atr) continue; const uh = P.ph1[lastLE(P.ph1, i - cfg.right - 1)], ul = P.pl1[lastLE(P.pl1, i - cfg.right - 1)]; const okH = uh !== undefined && uh >= s + cfg.left && uh <= e - cfg.right, okL = ul !== undefined && ul >= s + cfg.left && ul <= e - cfg.right; for (const kind of ["DEMAND", "SUPPLY"]) { const isD = kind === "DEMAND"; if (isD ? !(C[i] > O[i] && okH && C[i-1] <= H[uh] && C[i] > H[uh]) : !(C[i] < O[i] && okL && C[i-1] >= L[ul] && C[i] < L[ul])) continue; let src = -1; for (let k = i - 1; k >= Math.max(s, i - cfg.search); k--) if (isD ? C[k] < O[k] : C[k] > O[k]) { src = k; break; } if (src < 0) continue; const lo = isD ? L[src] : Math.min(O[src], C[src]), hi = isD ? Math.max(O[src], C[src]) : H[src]; let invalid = false; for (let k = i + 1; k <= e; k++) if (isD ? C[k] < lo : C[k] > hi) { invalid = true; break; } if (invalid || lo >= hi) continue; const avail = t0 + i*H1 + H1; if (lastOpen - avail > cfg.maxAge*H1) continue; zones.push({ kind, lo, hi, src, bos: i, avail }); } } zones.sort((a, b) => b.bos - a.bos || b.src - a.src); res = { trend, zones }; } P.zmemo.set(e, res); if (P.zmemo.size > 5000) P.zmemo.clear(); return res; } // first 5m index (>= avail) whose range touches the zone function firstTouch(P, z) { const key = z.kind + z.bos + ":" + z.lo + ":" + z.hi; if (P.touch.has(key)) return P.touch.get(key); const m = P.m5; let j = Math.ceil((z.avail - m.t0)/M5), r = Infinity; for (j = Math.max(0, j); j < P.n5; j++) if (m.l[j] <= z.hi && m.h[j] >= z.lo) { r = j; break; } P.touch.set(key, r); return r; } function decide(P, j, used) { const cfg = P.cfg, m = P.m5, s5 = Math.max(0, j - cfg.W5 + 1); if (j - s5 + 1 < Math.max(60, cfg.atrP + cfg.left + cfg.right + 5)) return null; const cutoff = m.t0 + (j+1)*M5, e = Math.floor((cutoff - P.h1.t0)/H1) - 1; if (e < 0 || e >= P.h1.n) return null; const st = h1State(P, e); if (!st || st.trend === "NEUTRAL") return null; const atr5 = j - s5 >= cfg.atrP ? smaAtr(m.o, m.h, m.l, m.c, j, cfg.atrP) : null; if (atr5 === null || !(atr5 > 0)) return null; const bull = st.trend === "BULLISH", arr = bull ? P.ph5 : P.pl5, q = lastLE(arr, j - cfg.right - 1); if (q < 0 || arr[q] < s5 + cfg.left) return null; const level = bull ? m.h[arr[q]] : m.l[arr[q]], want = bull ? "DEMAND" : "SUPPLY"; const o = m.o[j], c = m.c[j], body = Math.abs(c - o); for (const z of st.zones) { if (z.kind !== want) continue; if (m.t0 + s5*M5 > z.avail) continue; const ft = firstTouch(P, z), zk = z.kind + z.bos + ":" + z.lo + ":" + z.hi; if (!cfg.confirmBars) { if (ft !== j) continue; } else { // نسخة مخففة: التأكيد خلال confirmBars شمعة بعد أول لمسة، بشرط ما تسكّر شمعة ورا المنطقة قبلها، ومرة وحدة لكل منطقة if (!(ft <= j && j <= ft + cfg.confirmBars) || (used && used.has(zk))) continue; let broke = false; for (let k = ft; k < j; k++) if (bull ?
